624	def _subagent_appendix(filename: str) -> str:
625	    content = resources.files("megaplan").joinpath("data", filename).read_text(encoding="utf-8")
626	    content = content.replace(
627	        "{max_execute_no_progress}",
628	        str(get_effective("execution", "max_execute_no_progress")),
629	    )
630	    content = content.replace(
631	        "{max_review_rework_cycles}",
632	        str(get_effective("execution", "max_review_rework_cycles")),
633	    )
634	    return content
635	
636	
637	def _claude_subagent_appendix() -> str:
638	    return _subagent_appendix("claude_subagent_appendix.md")
639	
640	
641	def _codex_subagent_appendix() -> str:
642	    return _subagent_appendix("codex_subagent_appendix.md")
643	
644	
645	def bundled_global_file(name: str) -> str:
646	    content = _canonical_instructions()
647	    if name == "claude_skill.md":
648	        return _SKILL_HEADER + content + "\n\n" + _claude_subagent_appendix()
649	    if name == "codex_skill.md":
650	        return _SKILL_HEADER + content + "\n\n" + _codex_subagent_appendix()
651	    if name == "skill.md":
652	        return _SKILL_HEADER + content
653	    if name == "cursor_rule.mdc":
654	        return _CURSOR_HEADER + content
655	    return content
656	
657	
658	_GLOBAL_TARGETS = [
659	    {"agent": "claude", "detect": ".claude", "path": ".claude/skills/megaplan/SKILL.md", "data": "claude_skill.md"},
660	    {"agent": "codex", "detect": ".codex", "path": ".codex/skills/megaplan/SKILL.md", "data": "codex_skill.md"},
661	    {"agent": "cursor", "detect": ".cursor", "path": ".cursor/rules/megaplan.mdc", "data": "cursor_rule.mdc"},
662	]
663	
664	
665	def _install_owned_file(path: Path, content: str, *, force: bool = False) -> dict[str, bool | str]:
666	    existed = path.exists()
667	    if existed and not force:
668	        if path.read_text(encoding="utf-8") == content:
669	            return {"path": str(path), "skipped": True, "existed": True}
670	    atomic_write_text(path, content)
671	    return {"path": str(path), "skipped": False, "existed": existed}
672	
673	
674	def handle_setup_global(force: bool = False, home: Path | None = None) -> StepResponse:
675	    if home is None:
676	        home = Path.home()
677	    installed: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
678	    detected_count = 0
679	    for target in _GLOBAL_TARGETS:
680	        agent_dir = home / target["detect"]
681	        if not agent_dir.is_dir():
682	            installed.append({"agent": target["agent"], "path": str(home / target["path"]), "skipped": True, "reason": "not installed"})
683	            continue
684	        detected_count += 1
685	        result = _install_owned_file(home / target["path"], bundled_global_file(target["data"]), force=force)
686	        result["agent"] = target["agent"]
687	        installed.append(result)
688	    if detected_count == 0:
689	        return {
690	            "success": False, "step": "setup", "mode": "global",
691	            "summary": "No supported agents detected. Create one of ~/.claude/, ~/.codex/, or ~/.cursor/ and re-run.",
692	            "installed": installed,
693	        }
694	    available = detect_available_agents()
695	    config_path = None
696	    routing = None
697	    if available:
698	        agents_config = {step: (default if default in available else available[0]) for step, default in DEFAULT_AGENT_ROUTING.items()}
699	        config = load_config(home)
700	        config["agents"] = agents_config
701	        config_path = save_config(config, home)
702	        routing = agents_config
703	    lines = []
704	    for rec in installed:
705	        if rec.get("reason") == "not installed":
706	            lines.append(f"  {rec['agent']}: skipped (not installed)")
707	        elif rec["skipped"]:
708	            lines.append(f"  {rec['agent']}: up to date")
709	        else:
710	            lines.append(f"  {rec['agent']}: {'overwrote' if rec['existed'] else 'created'} {rec['path']}")
711	    result_data: dict[str, Any] = {"success": True, "step": "setup", "mode": "global", "summary": "Global setup complete:\n" + "\n".join(lines), "installed": installed}
712	    if config_path is not None:
713	        result_data["config_path"] = str(config_path)
714	        result_data["routing"] = routing
715	    return result_data
716	
717	
718	def handle_setup(args: argparse.Namespace) -> StepResponse:
719	    local = args.local or args.target_dir
720	    if not local:
721	        return handle_setup_global(force=args.force)
722	    target_dir = Path(args.target_dir).resolve() if args.target_dir else Path.cwd()
723	    target = target_dir / "AGENTS.md"
724	    content = bundled_agents_md()
725	    if target.exists() and not args.force:
726	        existing = target.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
727	        if "megaplan" in existing.lower():
728	            return {"success": True, "step": "setup", "summary": f"AGENTS.md already contains megaplan instructions at {target}", "skipped": True}
729	        atomic_write_text(target, existing + "\n\n" + content)
730	        return {"success": True, "step": "setup", "summary": f"Appended megaplan instructions to existing {target}", "file": str(target)}
731	    atomic_write_text(target, content)
732	    return {"success": True, "step": "setup", "summary": f"Created {target}", "file": str(target)}
733	
734	
735	def handle_config(args: argparse.Namespace) -> StepResponse:
736	    action = args.config_action
737	    if action == "show":
738	        config = load_config()
739	        effective_routing = {step: config.get("agents", {}).get(step, default) for step, default in DEFAULT_AGENT_ROUTING.items()}
740	        effective_settings = {
741	            dot_key: get_effective(section, setting)
742	            for dot_key in sorted(DEFAULTS)
743	            for section, setting in [dot_key.split(".", 1)]
744	        }
745	        return {
746	            "success": True,
747	            "step": "config",
748	            "action": "show",
749	            "config_path": str(config_dir() / "config.json"),
750	            "routing": effective_routing,
751	            "effective_settings": effective_settings,
752	            "raw_config": config,
753	        }
754	    if action == "set":
755	        key, value = args.key, args.value
756	        parts = key.split(".", 1)
757	        config = load_config()
758	        valid_keys = [
759	            *(f"agents.{step}" for step in DEFAULT_AGENT_ROUTING),
760	            "orchestration.mode",
761	            *sorted(_SETTABLE_BOOL),
762	            *sorted(_SETTABLE_ENUM),
763	            *sorted(_SETTABLE_NUMERIC),
764	        ]
765	        if len(parts) != 2:
766	            raise CliError(
767	                "invalid_args",
768	                f"Unknown config key '{key}'. Valid keys: {', '.join(valid_keys)}",
769	            )
770	        section, setting = parts
771	        normalized_value = value.strip().lower()
772	        if section == "agents":
773	            if setting not in DEFAULT_AGENT_ROUTING:
774	                raise CliError("invalid_args", f"Unknown step '{setting}'. Valid steps: {', '.join(DEFAULT_AGENT_ROUTING)}")
775	            if value not in KNOWN_AGENTS:
776	                raise CliError("invalid_args", f"Unknown agent '{value}'. Valid agents: {', '.join(KNOWN_AGENTS)}")
777	            config.setdefault("agents", {})[setting] = value
778	        elif key == "orchestration.mode":
779	            if value not in {"inline", "subagent"}:
780	                raise CliError("invalid_args", "orchestration.mode must be 'inline' or 'subagent'")
781	            config.setdefault("orchestration", {})["mode"] = value
782	        elif key in _SETTABLE_BOOL:
783	            if normalized_value in {"true", "1", "yes", "on"}:
784	                parsed_value = True
785	            elif normalized_value in {"false", "0", "no", "off"}:
786	                parsed_value = False
787	            else:
788	                raise CliError(
789	                    "invalid_args",
790	                    f"{key} must be one of: true, false, 1, 0, yes, no, on, off",
791	                )
792	            config.setdefault(section, {})[setting] = parsed_value
793	        elif key in _SETTABLE_ENUM:
794	            allowed_values = _SETTABLE_ENUM[key]
795	            if value not in allowed_values:
796	                raise CliError(
797	                    "invalid_args",
798	                    f"{key} must be one of: {', '.join(allowed_values)}",
799	                )
800	            config.setdefault(section, {})[setting] = value
801	        elif key in _SETTABLE_NUMERIC:
802	            try:
803	                parsed_value = int(value)
804	            except ValueError as exc:
805	                raise CliError("invalid_args", f"{key} must be an integer, got '{value}'") from exc
806	            config.setdefault(section, {})[setting] = parsed_value
807	        else:
808	            raise CliError(
809	                "invalid_args",
810	                f"Unknown config key '{key}'. Valid keys: {', '.join(valid_keys)}",
811	            )
812	        save_config(config)
813	        return {"success": True, "step": "config", "action": "set", "key": key, "value": config[section][setting]}
814	    if action == "reset":
815	        path = config_dir() / "config.json"
816	        if path.exists():
817	            path.unlink()
818	        return {"success": True, "step": "config", "action": "reset", "summary": "Config file removed. Using defaults."}
819	    raise CliError("invalid_args", f"Unknown config action: {action}")
820

826	def build_parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
827	    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Megaplan orchestration CLI")
828	    subparsers = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
829	
830	    setup_parser = subparsers.add_parser("setup", help="Install megaplan into agent configs (global by default)")
831	    setup_parser.add_argument("--local", action="store_true", help="Install AGENTS.md into a project instead of global agent configs")
832	    setup_parser.add_argument("--target-dir", help="Directory to install into (default: cwd, implies --local)")
833	    setup_parser.add_argument("--force", action="store_true", help="Overwrite existing files")
834	
835	    init_parser = subparsers.add_parser("init")

839	    init_parser.add_argument("--robustness", choices=list(ROBUSTNESS_LEVELS), default=None)
840	    init_parser.add_argument("--mode", choices=["code", "doc", "metaplan"], default=None,
841	                             help="Deliverable type: 'code' (source changes) or 'doc' / 'metaplan' "
842	                                  "(design/spec artifact — 'metaplan' is an alias for 'doc'). "
843	                                  "Defaults to 'code' unless the idea strongly suggests a design document, "
844	                                  "in which case --mode must be passed explicitly.")

846	                             help="Relative path where the doc artifact will be written. "

849	                             help="Use Hermes agent for all phases. Optional: specify default model")
850	    init_parser.add_argument("--phase-model", action="append", default=[],
851	                             help="Per-phase model override: --phase-model critique=hermes:openai/gpt-5")
852	    init_parser.add_argument("idea")

854	    list_parser = subparsers.add_parser("list")
855	    list_parser.add_argument("--all", action="store_true",

858	                             help="Only show plans from the current directory (default includes parent + child)")

863	    list_parser.add_argument("--summary", action="store_true",

865	
866	    for name in ["status", "audit", "progress", "watch"]:

870	            step_parser.add_argument("--pending-human", action="store_true",
871	                                     help="List plans awaiting human verification")
872	
873	    for name in ["plan", "prep", "critique", "revise", "gate", "finalize", "execute", "review"]:

966	    loop_init_parser.add_argument("--phase-model", action="append", default=[],

1061	def _find_megaplan_root(start: Path) -> Path:
1062	    """Walk up from *start* to find the git-root directory containing ``.megaplan/``.
1063	
1064	    Strategy: find the git root first (like ``git rev-parse --show-toplevel``),
1065	    then check if it has a ``.megaplan/`` directory.  This avoids ambiguity when
1066	    nested subdirectories also have their own ``.megaplan/``.  Falls back to the
1067	    nearest ancestor with ``.megaplan/`` if not in a git repo, and finally to
1068	    *start* if nothing is found.
1069	    """
1070	    resolved = start.resolve()
1071	
1072	    # Try git root first — the canonical project root.
1073	    git_root = _find_git_root(resolved)
1074	    if git_root and (git_root / ".megaplan").is_dir():
1075	        return git_root
1076	
1077	    # Fallback: walk up to find nearest .megaplan
1078	    current = resolved
1079	    while True:
1080	        if (current / ".megaplan").is_dir():
1081	            return current
1082	        parent = current.parent
1083	        if parent == current:
1084	            return start
1085	        current = parent
1086	
1087	
1088	def _find_git_root(start: Path) -> Path | None:
1089	    """Walk up to find the directory containing ``.git``."""
1090	    current = start
1091	    while True:
1092	        if (current / ".git").exists():
1093	            return current
1094	        parent = current.parent
1095	        if parent == current:
1096	            return None
1097	        current = parent
1098	
1099	
1100	def _auto_sync_installed_skills() -> None:
1101	    try:
1102	        for target in _GLOBAL_TARGETS:
1103	            agent_dir = Path.home() / target["detect"]
1104	            if not agent_dir.is_dir():
1105	                continue
1106	            _install_owned_file(Path.home() / target["path"], bundled_global_file(target["data"]), force=False)
1107	    except Exception:
1108	        pass
1109	
1110	
1111	def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
1112	    parser = build_parser()
1113	    args, remaining = parser.parse_known_args(argv)
1114	    if args.command != "setup":
1115	        _auto_sync_installed_skills()
1116	    try:
1117	        if args.command == "setup":
1118	            return render_response(handle_setup(args))
1119	        if args.command == "config":
1120	            return render_response(handle_config(args))
1121	    except CliError as error:
1122	        return error_response(error)
1123	
1124	    # Capture the working directory for subprocess workers (--add-dir / -C).
1125	    # This preserves git-worktree isolation: workers edit source code under
1126	    # the CWD (or an explicit --work-dir override), not the plan's stored
1127	    # project_dir (which may be a sibling checkout).
1128	    from megaplan.workers import set_work_dir_override
1129	    work_dir_override = getattr(args, "work_dir", None)
1130	    set_work_dir_override(work_dir_override if work_dir_override else Path.cwd())
1131	
1132	    root = _find_megaplan_root(Path.cwd())
1133	    ensure_runtime_layout(root)
1134	
1135	    if args.command == "auto":
1136	        from megaplan.auto import run_auto
1137	        try:
1138	            return run_auto(root, args)
1139	        except CliError as error:
1140	            return error_response(error, root=root)
1141	
1142	    if args.command == "chain":
1143	        from megaplan.chain import run_chain_cli
1144	        try:
1145	            return run_chain_cli(root, args)
1146	        except CliError as error:
1147	            return error_response(error, root=root)
1148	
1149	    if args.command == "cloud":
1150	        from megaplan.cloud.cli import run_cloud_cli
1151	        try:
1152	            return run_cloud_cli(root, args)
1153	        except CliError as error:
1154	            return error_response(error, root=root)
1155	
1156	    if args.command == "tiebreaker":
1157	        from megaplan.tiebreaker import run_tiebreaker_cli
1158	        try:
1159	            return run_tiebreaker_cli(root, args)
1160	        except CliError as error:
1161	            return error_response(error, root=root)
1162	
1163	    try:
1164	        handler = COMMAND_HANDLERS.get(args.command)
1165	        if handler is None:
1166	            raise CliError("invalid_command", f"Unknown command {args.command!r}")
1167	        if args.command == "override" and remaining:
1168	            if not args.note:
1169	                args.note = " ".join(remaining)
1170	            remaining = []
1171	        if remaining:
1172	            parser.error(f"unrecognized arguments: {' '.join(remaining)}")
1173	        if args.command == "override" and args.override_action == "add-note" and not args.note:
1174	            raise CliError("invalid_args", "override add-note requires a note")
1175	        if args.command == "override" and args.override_action == "set-robustness" and not args.robustness:
1176	            raise CliError("invalid_args", f"override set-robustness requires --robustness {'|'.join(ROBUSTNESS_LEVELS)}")
1177	        return render_response(handler(root, args))
1178	    except CliError as error:
1179	        return error_response(error, root=root)
1180	
1181	
1182	if __name__ == "__main__":
1183	    sys.exit(main())
1184

1	# Cloud runner spec for `megaplan cloud`.
2	#
3	# provider:
4	#   Sprint 1 only supports Railway. Sprint 2 will add fly, ssh, and local.
5	provider: railway
6	
7	# repo:
8	#   url      Git repository cloned into the persistent workspace volume.
9	#   branch   Branch checked out on first boot.
10	#   workspace
11	#            Absolute path inside the container where the repo lives.
12	repo:
13	  url: https://github.com/example/app.git
14	  branch: main
15	  workspace: /workspace/app
16	
17	# agents:
18	#   `default` applies to every megaplan step. Add per-step overrides only when
19	#   a specific phase should use a different agent.
20	agents:
21	  default: codex
22	  # execute: codex
23	  # review: codex
24	
25	# codex:
26	#   Written to /root/.codex/config.toml on every boot.
27	codex:
28	  model: gpt-5.4
29	  reasoning: high
30	
31	# mode behavior:
32	#   | mode  | agent session boot action                                  | required fields                    |
33	#   | auto  | init plan if missing, then run `megaplan auto --plan ...` | auto.plan_name, auto.idea_file     |
34	#   | chain | run `megaplan chain --spec ...`                            | chain.spec                         |
35	#   | idle  | start `bash -l` only                                       | none                               |
36	#   Missing auto.idea_file or chain.spec is a warning path in sprint 1: the
37	#   container drops to idle instead of crashing so you can upload files manually.
38	mode: idle
39	
40	# auto:
41	#   Required only when mode=auto.
42	auto:
43	  plan_name: my-plan
44	  idea_file: /workspace/idea.txt
45	  robustness: standard
46	
47	# chain:
48	#   Required only when mode=chain.
49	chain:
50	  spec: /workspace/chain.yaml
51	
52	# megaplan:
53	#   Branch/tag/SHA used for the boot-time `pip install --upgrade` of megaplan.

55	  ref: main
56	
57	# resources:

61	  volume: agent-volume
62	  port: 8080
63	
64	# railway:

728	def handle_init(root: Path, args: argparse.Namespace) -> StepResponse:
729	    ensure_runtime_layout(root)
730	    project_dir = Path(args.project_dir).expanduser().resolve()
731	    if not project_dir.exists() or not project_dir.is_dir():
732	        raise CliError("invalid_project_dir", f"Project directory does not exist: {project_dir}")
733	    explicit_mode = getattr(args, "mode", None)
734	    raw_output_path = getattr(args, "output", None)
735	    mode = explicit_mode or "code"
736	    if mode == "metaplan":
737	        mode = "doc"
738	
739	    if mode == "code" and raw_output_path:
740	        raise CliError(
741	            "invalid_args",
742	            "--output is only valid with --mode doc. For code-mode runs, remove "
743	            "--output; for design-document runs, also pass --mode doc.",
744	        )
745	    normalized_output_path: str | None = None
746	    if mode == "doc" and not raw_output_path:
747	        raise CliError("invalid_args", "--output is required when --mode doc is selected")
748	    if raw_output_path:
749	        output_candidate = Path(raw_output_path)
750	        if output_candidate.is_absolute():
751	            raise CliError("invalid_args", "--output must be a relative path inside the project directory")
752	        if any(part == ".." for part in output_candidate.parts):
753	            raise CliError("invalid_args", "--output must not contain '..' path traversal")
754	        resolved_output_path = (project_dir / output_candidate).resolve()
755	        try:

757	        except ValueError as exc:

1	"""Auto-driver that advances a plan through its phases without human intervention.
2	
3	This is the mechanical loop that most orchestrators end up writing by hand:
4	read `status`, run `next_step`, repeat until terminal. All real judgment is
5	delegated to megaplan's existing phase logic — the driver only applies two
6	documented defaults:
7	
8	1. Gate ESCALATE → force-proceed (caller opts out with ``--on-escalate abort``
9	   or ``--on-escalate fail``).
10	2. Same state for N consecutive iterations → bail (stall detection).
11	
12	The driver is intentionally dumb. If a run needs judgment the driver can't
13	provide, it exits with a non-zero status and prints the state snapshot so the
14	caller can intervene.
15	"""
16	from __future__ import annotations
17	
18	import argparse
19	import json
20	import subprocess
21	import sys
22	import time
23	from dataclasses import dataclass, field
24	from pathlib import Path
25	from typing import Any
26	
27	from megaplan.types import (
28	    AUTOMATION_TERMINAL_STATES,
29	    STATE_AWAITING_HUMAN,
30	    STATE_TIEBREAKER_PENDING,
31	    STATE_TIEBREAKER_READY,
32	    TERMINAL_STATES,
33	)
34	
35	
36	DEFAULT_STALL_THRESHOLD = 5
37	DEFAULT_MAX_ITERATIONS = 200
38	DEFAULT_POLL_SLEEP_SECONDS = 1.0
39	DEFAULT_PHASE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 3600
40	DEFAULT_STATUS_TIMEOUT_SECONDS = 60
41	# Cap on review→rework cycles before the driver bails. This mirrors the
42	# `execution.max_review_rework_cycles` config the review handler enforces
43	# internally (default 3); the auto-driver applies its own cap so that an
44	# unexpected-config or mis-routed rework loop cannot spin indefinitely.
45	DEFAULT_MAX_REVIEW_REWORK_CYCLES = 3
46	ESCALATE_ACTIONS = ("force-proceed", "abort", "fail")
47	PHASE_TIMEOUT_EXIT_CODE = 124  # conventional; matches GNU `timeout`
48	
49	
50	@dataclass
51	class DriverOutcome:
52	    """Terminal outcome reported when the loop exits."""
53	
54	    status: str  # "done" | "stalled" | "escalated" | "failed" | "aborted" | "cap" | "blocked"
55	    plan: str
56	    final_state: str
57	    iterations: int
58	    reason: str = ""
59	    last_phase: str | None = None
60	    events: list[dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=list)
61	
62	    def to_json(self) -> str:
63	        return json.dumps(
64	            {
65	                "status": self.status,
66	                "plan": self.plan,
67	                "final_state": self.final_state,
68	                "iterations": self.iterations,
69	                "reason": self.reason,
70	                "last_phase": self.last_phase,
71	                "events": self.events,
72	            },
73	            indent=2,
74	        )
75	
76	
77	def _run_megaplan(
78	    args: list[str],
79	    *,
80	    cwd: Path | None = None,
81	    timeout: float | None = None,
82	) -> tuple[int, str, str]:
83	    """Run a megaplan sub-command in its own process.
84	
85	    We shell out rather than importing the handlers directly so each phase gets
86	    a fresh argparse/handler lifecycle. This matches how external orchestrators
87	    drive the CLI and avoids subtle state leakage between phases.
88	
89	    ``timeout`` is seconds to wait before killing the subprocess. On timeout we
90	    return exit code ``PHASE_TIMEOUT_EXIT_CODE`` and append a marker to stderr
91	    so the driver can surface it as a phase failure without crashing the loop.
92	    The subprocess is killed; any grandchildren it spawned (e.g. codex) may
93	    need a moment to settle but will exit when their parent's pipes close.
94	    """
95	    try:
96	        proc = subprocess.run(
97	            ["megaplan", *args],
98	            cwd=str(cwd) if cwd else None,
99	            capture_output=True,
100	            text=True,
101	            check=False,
102	            timeout=timeout,
103	        )
104	        return proc.returncode, proc.stdout, proc.stderr
105	    except subprocess.TimeoutExpired as expired:
106	        stdout = expired.stdout if isinstance(expired.stdout, str) else ""
107	        stderr = expired.stderr if isinstance(expired.stderr, str) else ""
108	        marker = f"\n[megaplan auto] subprocess timed out after {timeout}s"
109	        return PHASE_TIMEOUT_EXIT_CODE, stdout, (stderr + marker).strip()
110	
111	
112	def _status(
113	    plan: str,
114	    cwd: Path | None = None,
115	    *,
116	    timeout: float = DEFAULT_STATUS_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
117	) -> dict[str, Any]:
118	    code, out, err = _run_megaplan(["status", "--plan", plan], cwd=cwd, timeout=timeout)
119	    if code != 0:
120	        raise RuntimeError(f"megaplan status failed (exit {code}): {err.strip() or out.strip()}")
```

484	def build_auto_parser(subparsers: Any) -> None:
485	    auto_parser = subparsers.add_parser(
486	        "auto",
487	        help="Drive a plan to completion without human intervention",
488	    )
489	    auto_parser.add_argument("--plan", required=True, help="Plan name")
490	    auto_parser.add_argument(
491	        "--stall-threshold",
492	        type=int,
493	        default=DEFAULT_STALL_THRESHOLD,
494	        help=(
495	            f"Exit if the plan state doesn't change for this many iterations "
496	            f"AND no new review.json has been written (default "
497	            f"{DEFAULT_STALL_THRESHOLD}). Use --max-review-rework-cycles for "
498	            "the rework-loop limit — execute rework can span many iterations "
499	            "with state pinned at 'finalized', which is not a real stall."
500	        ),
501	    )
502	    auto_parser.add_argument(
503	        "--max-iterations",
504	        type=int,
505	        default=DEFAULT_MAX_ITERATIONS,
506	        help=f"Hard cap on loop iterations (default {DEFAULT_MAX_ITERATIONS})",
507	    )
508	    auto_parser.add_argument(
509	        "--max-review-rework-cycles",
510	        type=int,
511	        default=DEFAULT_MAX_REVIEW_REWORK_CYCLES,
512	        help=(
513	            f"Cap on observed review→rework cycles before the driver bails "
514	            f"(default {DEFAULT_MAX_REVIEW_REWORK_CYCLES}). A rework cycle is "
515	            "counted each time review.json is rewritten while state appears "
516	            "stuck at 'finalized'. Mirrors execution.max_review_rework_cycles."
517	        ),
518	    )
519	    auto_parser.add_argument(
520	        "--on-escalate",
521	        choices=ESCALATE_ACTIONS,
522	        default="force-proceed",
523	        help="What to do when the gate escalates (default force-proceed)",
524	    )
525	    auto_parser.add_argument(
526	        "--poll-sleep",
527	        type=float,
528	        default=DEFAULT_POLL_SLEEP_SECONDS,
529	        help=f"Seconds to sleep between phase transitions (default {DEFAULT_POLL_SLEEP_SECONDS})",
530	    )
531	    auto_parser.add_argument(
532	        "--phase-timeout",
533	        type=float,
534	        default=DEFAULT_PHASE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
535	        help=(
536	            f"Seconds before a single phase subprocess (plan/prep/critique/gate/finalize/execute/review) "
537	            f"is killed and treated as a failure (default {DEFAULT_PHASE_TIMEOUT_SECONDS}s). "
538	            "Stall detection still applies on top."
539	        ),
540	    )
541	    auto_parser.add_argument(
542	        "--work-dir",
543	        default=None,
544	        help=(
545	            "Override the source-code working directory for subprocess workers "
546	            "(--add-dir / -C). Defaults to the current working directory."
547	        ),
548	    )
549	    auto_parser.add_argument(
550	        "--status-timeout",
551	        type=float,
552	        default=DEFAULT_STATUS_TIMEOUT_SECONDS,
553	        help=(
554	            f"Seconds before `megaplan status` / override subprocesses are killed "
555	            f"(default {DEFAULT_STATUS_TIMEOUT_SECONDS}s). These should always be quick; "
556	            "hitting this indicates serious trouble."
557	        ),
558	    )
559	
560	
561	def run_auto(root: Path, args: argparse.Namespace) -> int:
562	    """CLI entry point. Returns a POSIX exit code suitable for ``sys.exit``."""
563	    outcome = drive(
564	        args.plan,
565	        cwd=root,
566	        stall_threshold=args.stall_threshold,
567	        max_iterations=args.max_iterations,
568	        max_review_rework_cycles=args.max_review_rework_cycles,
569	        on_escalate=args.on_escalate,
570	        poll_sleep=args.poll_sleep,
571	        phase_timeout=args.phase_timeout,
572	        status_timeout=args.status_timeout,
573	    )
574	    sys.stdout.write(outcome.to_json() + "\n")
575	    if outcome.status == "done":
576	        return 0
577	    if outcome.status == "aborted":
578	        return 0  # user-requested abort is not a failure
579	    if outcome.status == "stalled":
580	        return 2
581	    if outcome.status == "escalated":
582	        return 3
583	    if outcome.status == "cap":
584	        return 4
585	    # rc=3 is already claimed by 'escalated'; use rc=5 for all-blocked so the
586	    # supervisor can distinguish "workers said every task is blocked" from a
587	    # generic stall or escalation.
588	    if outcome.status == "blocked":
589	        return 5
590	    return 1
591

1	# Changelog
2	
3	## v0.19.0 — 2026-04-21
4	
5	### Built-in cloud runner via `megaplan cloud`
6	
7	Generalises the ad-hoc Railway container setup (previously external, at `~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev/`) into a built-in subcommand. Sprint 1 of two; scope is operational parity with the existing Railway flow.
8	
9	- **New subcommand group**: `megaplan cloud {init,build,deploy,status,attach,logs,exec,resume,down,destroy}` covers the full lifecycle of a provider-backed run.
10	- **Subpackage layout** under `megaplan/cloud/`: `spec.py` (cloud.yaml loader + schema validation), `template.py` (renders `entrypoint.sh` / `railway.toml` / `cloud.yaml`), `cli.py` (argparse wiring, dispatched from `megaplan.cli`).
11	- **Provider plugin model**: `providers/` ships a `Provider` ABC plus a Railway plugin that wraps the `railway` CLI. Non-Railway providers (ssh, local) are explicit non-goals for this sprint.
12	- **Bundled templates**: `megaplan/cloud/templates/` ships `Dockerfile`, `entrypoint.sh.tmpl`, `healthserver.py`, `railway.toml.tmpl`, `cloud.yaml.tmpl`, and `chain.yaml.example`.
13	- **Extracted run wrappers**: `megaplan/cloud/wrappers/` ships `mp-run`, `mp-supervise`, `mp-heartbeat`, and `mp-chain` bash scripts, lifted out of the old entrypoint heredocs so they are versioned and editable.
14	- **`handle_init` strictness**: `--output` on a code-mode plan now raises `invalid_args` instead of being silently accepted. The only legitimate use of `--output` was a dropped `--mode doc` flag, which is now an explicit error.
15	- **Discoverability**: README gains a short "Cloud runs" section, `megaplan/data/instructions.md` gains a "Cloud Mode" block so the skill teaches Claude Code / Codex when to suggest `megaplan cloud`, and `.gitignore` now covers `.env` / `.env.*` / `*.env` since the cloud flow encourages `OPENAI_API_KEY` / `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` / `GITHUB_TOKEN` secrets.
16	- **Out of scope for sprint 1**: ssh/local providers, `cloud init-plan` bootstrap, toolchain extensibility block, retiring the external reference folder. The existing `~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev/` keeps running unchanged.
17	
18	### `--mode metaplan` alias for doc mode
19	
20	- **CLI**: `megaplan init --mode metaplan` is now the preferred name for design/document runs. Internally it normalizes to `mode == "doc"`, so all existing state files, prompts, schemas, and downstream checks are untouched. `--mode doc` remains a valid alias.
21	- **Docs**: README section renamed to "Metaplan mode," with the defensive "Looking for metaplan/preplan?" subsection merged in now that metaplan is the real name. The SWE-bench Experiment section was dropped. Skill instructions updated to match.
22	
23	### Doc-mode aggregator preserves executor output
24	
25	- **Bug**: `assemble_doc` was overwriting the executor-authored output file with `executor_notes` (verification prose), so every doc-mode run ended with status-summary text instead of the intended deliverable. The executor is the authoritative author — the aggregator's only job is to fall back when the executor couldn't write to disk.
26	- **Fix**: `assemble_doc` now returns untouched if the output file exists with non-empty content. Empty or missing files still fall through to the degraded notes-based path; a docstring callout flags that its content is verification prose, not authored sections. Regression tests cover both branches.
27	
28	### Misc
29	
30	- Doc-mode / metaplan pointer landed pre-alias: README "Doc mode" section, `instructions.md` Modes block with a keyword-loaded "Looking for metaplan or preplan mode?" subsection, and `claude_subagent_appendix.md` note about the `--mode doc --output` flags the outer skill appends.
31	- README prose cleanup (light-megaplan run; no factual or code-example changes).
32	- License correction: README had claimed MIT; actual license per `LICENSE` and `pyproject.toml` is Open Source Native License (OSNL) 0.2.
33	
34	## v0.18.1 — 2026-04-16
35	
36	### Rework-cycle-aware stall detection in `megaplan auto`
37	
38	Fixes a false-positive stall in the auto-driver when a plan is in a review→rework loop.
39	
40	- **Bug**: `megaplan auto` counted iterations at the same `state` and bailed after `--stall-threshold` (default 5). When review returns `needs_rework`, state ping-pongs `finalized ↔ executed ↔ finalized` while execute re-runs batches. From the naive stall counter's view the plan looked "stuck at finalized for 5 iterations" even though new `review.json` / `execution.json` artifacts were being written and real progress was happening. Observed on plan `milestone-m1b-from-docs-state-20260416-1226` — 7 execute batches + 1 review cycle completed, driver exited rc=2.
41	- **Fix**: auto's driver loop now tracks `review.json` mtime as a forward-progress marker. When the marker advances, the stall counter resets and a rework-cycle counter increments. The new `--max-review-rework-cycles` flag (default 3, mirroring `execution.max_review_rework_cycles`) caps runaway rework loops independently of `--stall-threshold`.
42	- **Helpers**: `_resolve_plan_dir(plan, cwd)` walks up from cwd to find `.megaplan/plans/<plan>`, matching how `megaplan status` resolves plans. `_get_review_marker(plan_dir)` stats `review.json` and returns `None` for plans without a review artifact (e.g. light-robustness plans) — in that case the driver falls back to plain stall detection, preserving existing behavior.
43	- **CLI**: `--stall-threshold` help text updated to clarify that it fires only when state AND `review.json` are both unchanged. New `--max-review-rework-cycles` flag added with matching default.
44	- **Tests**: `tests/test_auto.py` (new file) covers stall reset on review-marker advance, rework cap enforcement, plain-stall fallback when no review.json exists, and the plan-dir resolver.
45	- **Scope**: auto.py driver loop only — execute and review phase logic are untouched.
46	
47	## v0.18.0 — 2026-04-15
48	
49	### Automated tiebreaker triggering
50	
51	Gate-driven automatic tiebreaker routing with budgeted, audited guardrails. Builds on the advisory `megaplan tiebreaker` subcommand from v0.17.1 — the harness now detects recurring constraint tensions and routes to tiebreaker automatically.
52	
53	- **Iteration-pressure analysis**: `megaplan/iteration_pressure.py` computes flag recurrence history — fuzzy-groups flags by Jaccard word similarity, tracks `addressed_then_reopened_count`, and renders a pressure table into the gate prompt context.
54	- **Mechanical recurrence validation**: harness validates TIEBREAKER recommendations against actual flag history via `_validate_tiebreaker`. Re-prompts the gate once if no mechanical signal exists; force-demotes to ITERATE on second failure.
55	- **`tiebreaker-run` top-level command**: auto-driver-callable command that invokes `_run_tiebreaker` inside a plan-locked context and transitions the plan state.
56	- **Gate integration**: `handle_gate` detects `TIEBREAKER` recommendations, runs the validator, transitions to `tiebreaker_pending` on approval.
57	- **Budget guardrails**: `max_tiebreakers_per_plan` (default 2). Exceeded budgets force-demote to ESCALATE.
58	- **Domain blocklist**: `tiebreaker_blocklist` config field skips tiebreaker for specified concern categories.
59	- **Spec-level opt-out**: `allow_tiebreaker: false` in config disables the mechanism entirely.
60	- **Audit tracking**: `megaplan/audit.py` records tiebreaker usage, timing, and token costs. `megaplan tiebreaker audit` CLI for per-plan and global stats. `handle_tiebreaker_decide` writes an audit record on every decision.
61	- **Auto driver**: `drive()` returns `status="awaiting_human"`, `"tiebreaker_pending"`, or `"tiebreaker_ready"` for the matching automation-terminal states and stops cleanly.
62	- **Gate prompt**: now includes the Iteration Pressure Analysis block; TIEBREAKER bullet explicitly cites `addressed_then_reopened_count` thresholds.
63	
64	## v0.17.1 — 2026-04-15
65	
66	### Tiebreaker subcommand (`megaplan tiebreaker`)
67	
68	New advisory subcommand that produces structured decision context for architectural questions (e.g. when `gate` returns ESCALATE).
69	
70	- **Two-agent pipeline**: a researcher agent gathers evidence and options, then a challenger agent stress-tests the findings — each in an independent ephemeral session.
71	- **CLI**: `megaplan tiebreaker --plan <name> --question "..."` (or `--question-file`), `megaplan tiebreaker status`, `megaplan tiebreaker decide --pick/--escalate/--replan --rationale`.
72	- **Structured artifacts**: `tiebreaker_researcher.json`, `tiebreaker_challenger.json`, and a synthesized `tiebreaker.md` with decision-ready sections (options table, evidence summary, agreement/disagreement, fallback plan).
73	- **Idempotent**: re-runs produce versioned artifacts (`_v2`, `_v3`, …).
74	- **Configurable agents**: `agents.tiebreaker_researcher` and `agents.tiebreaker_challenger` in config, defaulting to codex.
75	- **Gate schema**: `TIEBREAKER` added as fourth recommendation, with `tiebreaker_question`, `tiebreaker_flag_ids`, `tiebreaker_fuzzy_group_id` optional fields. Gate prompt documents the new option.
76	- **Plan lifecycle**: `tiebreaker_pending` / `tiebreaker_ready` states added with workflow transitions; both are automation-terminal.
77	- **Settled-decision immunity**: critique and revise prompts read `tiebreaker_decisions.json` and instruct agents not to re-raise settled concerns without materially new evidence.
78	- **Hot-fix**: `_run_tiebreaker` uses `WorkerResult.payload` instead of the nonexistent `.success`/`.parsed`/`.error` attributes — caught by live-cloud smoke run.
79	
80	Automatic gate-driven tiebreaker routing (pressure analysis, budgeting, audit tracking) lands in v0.18.0.
81	
82	## v0.17.0 — 2026-04-15
83	
84	### Verifiability contracts
85	
86	Success criteria now declare which capabilities are needed to verify them, enabling pre-critique auditing and human-deferred verification.
87	
88	- **Capability registry**: closed set of 11 mechanism-shaped strings in `megaplan/capabilities.py` — 6 container (`run_shell`, `read_files`, `run_tests`, `parse_diff`, `read_build_output`, `run_linter`) and 5 human (`drive_browser`, `inspect_runtime_ui`, `observe_runtime_logs`, `subjective_judgment`, `verify_physical_device`).
89	- **`requires` field on success criteria**: optional `requires: [cap1, cap2]` on each criterion in plan/revise schemas. Defaults to `[]` for backward compatibility.
90	- **Pre-critique audit**: `megaplan/verifiability.py` validates that `requires` entries are known capabilities and that the union of worker capabilities can satisfy them. Synthetic `verifiability` flags are injected into the critique phase.
91	- **`deferred_human` verdict**: review criteria that require human-only capabilities are marked `deferred_human` instead of `fail` or `waived`.
92	- **`awaiting_human_verify` state**: plans with deferred human criteria enter this automation-terminal state instead of `done`.
93	- **`megaplan verify-human`**: CLI command to record human verification evidence and transition from `awaiting_human_verify` to `done`.
94	- **`megaplan audit-verifiability`**: CLI command to inspect capability coverage of a plan's criteria without changing state.
95	- **`megaplan status --pending-human`**: lists plans in `awaiting_human_verify` state.
96	- **Migration**: `requires` defaults to `[]` via schema default. Existing plans work unchanged.
97	
98	## v0.16.0 — 2026-04-15
99	
100	### Chain driver (`megaplan chain`)
101	
102	New first-class subcommand that drives an ordered pipeline of milestone plans described by a YAML spec. Replaces ad-hoc bash orchestration (`chain.sh`) so plan-state logic lives in megaplan instead of fragile shell polling.
103	
104	- **Spec-driven**: `megaplan chain --spec path/to/chain.yaml` reads milestones, optional seed plan, and failure/escalate policies from YAML.
105	- **Resumable**: progress is persisted to `chain_state.json` next to the spec (`current_milestone_index`, `current_plan_name`, `last_state`, `completed`). A relaunched process reads this file and picks up where the previous run stopped.
106	- **State-aware in the right layer**: each milestone is driven via the existing `megaplan.auto.drive` entry point, so phase selection (`plan → prep → critique → ... → review`) stays in megaplan. Shell wrappers no longer need to classify `next_step`.
107	- **`megaplan chain status --spec PATH`**: prints current chain progress without driving.
108	- **Failure/escalate policies**: `stop_chain` (default), `skip_milestone`, `retry_milestone`.
109	- **Seed handling**: if a seed plan is specified and not already in a terminal state, it is driven first under the same auto loop — fixing the gap where seed plans had no state-aware driver.
110	- **Validation**: up front, the chain driver checks every idea file exists and the seed plan (if set) resolves under the project root. Structured `invalid_spec` / `missing_idea_file` / `missing_seed_plan` errors.
111	- **`--no-git-refresh` flag**: suppresses the automatic `git checkout main && git pull` that runs before each milestone. Default: enabled (preserves existing CI/orchestrator behavior). Disable on developer checkouts where you do not want chain to stomp on the currently checked-out branch.
112	- **PyYAML** added as a runtime dependency.
113	
114	### Tests
115	
116	- `tests/test_chain.py` covers spec parsing, `chain status`, idea-file validation, seed-plan validation, happy-path execution (with `auto.drive` mocked), resume-from-`chain_state.json`, on-failure `stop_chain`, and `--no-git-refresh` suppression.
117

1	#!/usr/bin/env bash
2	set -euo pipefail
3	
4	LOG=/var/log/entrypoint.log
5	exec > >(tee -a "$$LOG") 2>&1
6	
7	echo "=== $$(date -Iseconds) entrypoint starting ==="
8	
9	mkdir -p /workspace /root/.ssh /root/.config/megaplan /root/.codex
10	chmod 700 /root/.ssh
11	
12	REPO_URL="$${REPO_URL:-${REPO_URL}}"
13	REPO_BRANCH="$${REPO_BRANCH:-${REPO_BRANCH}}"
14	WORKSPACE_PATH="${WORKSPACE_PATH}"
15	REPO_DIR="$$WORKSPACE_PATH"
16	MODE="${MODE}"
17	IDEA_FILE="${IDEA_FILE}"
18	CHAIN_SPEC="${CHAIN_SPEC}"
19	AUTO_PLAN_NAME="${AUTO_PLAN_NAME}"
20	
21	# Git identity
22	git config --global user.email "$${GIT_EMAIL:-${CODEX_EMAIL}}"
23	git config --global user.name "$${GIT_NAME:-Codex Agent}"
24	git config --global init.defaultBranch main
25	git config --global pull.rebase false
26	git config --global --add safe.directory "$$REPO_DIR"
27	
28	# GitHub credential helper if token present
29	if [[ -n "$${GITHUB_TOKEN:-}" ]]; then
30	  echo "GITHUB_TOKEN present — configuring git credentials"
31	  git config --global credential.helper store
32	  cat > /root/.git-credentials <<EOF
33	https://x-access-token:$${GITHUB_TOKEN}@github.com
34	EOF
35	  chmod 600 /root/.git-credentials
36	fi
37	
38	# Clone repo if missing (volume may already have it from a prior boot)
39	if [[ ! -d "$$REPO_DIR/.git" ]]; then
40	  echo "Cloning $$REPO_URL into $$REPO_DIR (branch: $$REPO_BRANCH)"
41	  if git clone --branch "$$REPO_BRANCH" "$$REPO_URL" "$$REPO_DIR" 2>&1 | tail -10; then
42	    echo "Clone complete."
43	  else
44	    echo "Clone failed — set GITHUB_TOKEN or clone manually."
45	  fi
46	else
47	  echo "$$REPO_DIR already present — skipping clone."
48	fi
49	
50	# Codex auth from env
51	if [[ -n "$${OPENAI_API_KEY:-}" ]] && [[ ! -f /root/.codex/auth.json ]]; then
52	  echo "Authenticating codex with OPENAI_API_KEY"
53	  printf '%s' "$$OPENAI_API_KEY" | codex login --with-api-key 2>&1 | tail -3 || true
54	fi
55	
56	${CLAUDE_AUTH_BLOCK}
57	
58	# Codex model + reasoning config — always overwrite on boot so new tiers propagate.
59	# sandbox_mode = "danger-full-access" stays on because the container is the sandbox.
60	cat > /root/.codex/config.toml <<EOF
61	model = "${CODEX_MODEL}"
62	model_reasoning_effort = "${CODEX_REASONING}"
63	sandbox_mode = "danger-full-access"
64	approval_policy = "never"
65	EOF
66	echo "Codex model: ${CODEX_MODEL}, reasoning: ${CODEX_REASONING}, sandbox: danger-full-access (container-sandboxed)"
67	
68	# Megaplan install: always re-pull on boot so entrypoint restarts pick up the
69	# requested ref without rebuilding the base image.
70	MEGAPLAN_REF="$${MEGAPLAN_REF:-${MEGAPLAN_REF}}"
71	echo "Installing/upgrading megaplan from github:$$MEGAPLAN_REF"
72	pip install --upgrade --no-cache-dir "git+https://github.com/peteromallet/megaplan.git@$$MEGAPLAN_REF" 2>&1 | tail -3
73	
74	echo "Configuring megaplan agent routing and execution defaults"
75	${AGENT_ROUTING_BLOCK}
76	megaplan config set execution.auto_approve true >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
77	megaplan config set execution.robustness "${ROBUSTNESS}" >/dev/null 2>&1 || true
78	
79	echo "Runner mode: $$MODE"
80	
81	# Launch heartbeat in its own tmux session.
82	if ! tmux has-session -t heartbeat 2>/dev/null; then
83	  tmux new-session -d -s heartbeat -c /workspace "bash -lc '/usr/local/bin/mp-heartbeat'"
84	  echo "heartbeat watchdog running in tmux session 'heartbeat'"
85	fi
86	
87	# Tiny health server so Railway keeps container alive
88	python3 /usr/local/bin/healthserver.py &
89	HEALTH_PID=$$!
90	echo "healthserver.py running as pid $$HEALTH_PID"
91	
92	# Ensure tmux session 'agent' exists. The renderer injects the mode-specific
93	# launch block here so auto/chain can warn-and-drop-to-idle on missing inputs.
94	if ! tmux has-session -t agent 2>/dev/null; then
95	${RUNNER_LAUNCH_BLOCK}
96	fi
97	echo "tmux 'agent' session ready — attach: railway ssh --session agent"
98	
99	echo "=== entrypoint idle ==="
100	wait "$$HEALTH_PID"
101
```

Emit a combined response with the init payload plus `auto_outcome` containing `DriverOutcome.to_json()`.\n3. **Import rule:** `auto` already imports core-only modules; `handlers.py` already imports from `auto` indirectly via `execution.py`, so no cloud coupling is introduced.\n\n### Step 4: Core tests (`tests/test_chain.py`, `tests/test_handle_init_doc_mode.py` or new `tests/test_handle_init_idea_file.py`)\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **Extend** `tests/test_chain.py` with:\n   - `test_chain_start_invokes_driver` — mocks `auto.drive`, asserts `run_chain_cli(... chain_action=\"start\", spec=...)` runs the same validator + driver path.\n   - `test_format_chain_status_pretty` — feeds synthetic `ChainSpec` + `ChainState` (seed done, 1 completed milestone, 1 in-progress, 2 remaining) and asserts the `current_milestone`/`completed`/`remaining`/`per_milestone` fields.\n2. **Add** `tests/test_handle_init_idea_file.py` with cases:\n   - `test_init_reads_idea_file` (file → state.idea)\n   - `test_init_rejects_both_positional_and_idea_file`\n   - `test_init_auto_start_invokes_drive` (mocked `auto.drive`, asserts called once with the new plan name and root)\n3. **Add** `tests/test_core_without_cloud.py` that `importlib.reload`s `megaplan.cli` / `megaplan.chain` / `megaplan.handlers` with `sys.modules[\"megaplan.cloud\"] = None` set to a raising sentinel, runs `megaplan chain status --spec` and `megaplan init --idea-file --auto-start` via `main(argv=...)`, asserts both succeed without ever importing `megaplan.cloud.*`.\n\n---\n\n## Phase 2: Provider plumbing + Local + SSH providers\n\n### Step 5: Unlock `local` and `ssh` providers in the spec loader (`megaplan/cloud/spec.py`)\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **Change** `load_spec` (`megaplan/cloud/spec.py:167-172`): drop the sprint-1 gate. Accept `\"railway\" | \"local\" | \"ssh\"`. Leave `\"fly\"` in a `future_providers` tuple with a distinct error message so users aren't silently left with unhelpful errors. Update `SPRINT_TWO_PROVIDERS` to `FUTURE_PROVIDERS = (\"fly\",)`.\n2. **Add** `LocalSpec` (fields: `compose_project: str = \"megaplan-cloud\"`, `workdir: str | None = None`) and `SshSpec` (fields: `host: str`, `user: str = \"root\"`, `port: int = 22`, `identity_file: str | None = None`, `remote_dir: str = \"/var/lib/megaplan-cloud\"`). Parse both in `load_spec` when the provider matches; otherwise keep them `None`.\n3. **Update** `CloudSpec` dataclass to add `local: LocalSpec | None = None` and `ssh: SshSpec | None = None`. Strict-additive — field order unchanged, all existing `CloudSpec(...)` call sites in tests keep working.\n4. **Update** existing tests in `tests/test_cloud_spec.py`: drop `test_load_spec_rejects_*_sprint_two_providers` (or rename to assert `\"fly\"` is still rejected). Add happy-path tests for `provider: local` and `provider: ssh` including required-field validation.\n\n### Step 6: `Provider` base + registry updates (`megaplan/cloud/providers/base.py`)\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **Add** two optional abstract hooks on `Provider`:\n   - `upload_file(self, src: Path, dest: str) -> None` — used by `cloud chain` / `cloud bootstrap` to push a spec or idea file to the remote workspace. Default implementation on the base class raises `CliError(\"not_implemented\", ...)`; Railway + SSH + Local override.\n   - `read_remote_file(self, path: str) -> str` — used by `cloud status --chain` to fetch `chain_state.json` text. Same default/override pattern.\n2. **Replace** the hardcoded `if name != \"railway\"` block (`base.py:52-54`) with a name→class registry dict, importing providers lazily to keep startup lean. Unknown names raise `CliError(\"invalid_spec\", f\"Unknown cloud provider '{name}'. Known: railway, local, ssh\")`.\n3. **Implement** `upload_file` / `read_remote_file` on `RailwayProvider` using `railway ssh --service ... -- \"cat > dest\"` (base64 round-trip for safety) and `railway ssh -- cat <path>` respectively.\n\n### Step 7: Local provider (`megaplan/cloud/providers/local.py`, `megaplan/cloud/templates/docker-compose.yaml.tmpl`)\n**Scope:** Medium\n1. **Create** `megaplan/cloud/templates/docker-compose.yaml.tmpl` — single `agent` service built from the materialized `Dockerfile`, bind-mounting a host workspace dir (`./workspace` inside the deploy dir), exposing `${PORT}`, env from a `.env` file the provider writes with the resolved secrets.\n2. **Create** `megaplan/cloud/providers/local.py::LocalProvider` implementing the full `Provider` ABC:\n   - `build` → `docker compose -p <proj> build`.\n   - `deploy` → write `.env` with secrets, then `docker compose -p <proj> up -d`.\n   - `ssh_exec` → `docker compose -p <proj> exec -T agent bash -lc '<command>'`.\n   - `attach` → `docker compose -p <proj> exec agent tmux attach -t agent` (falls through to `bash -l` if no tmux session).\n   - `logs` → `docker compose -p <proj> logs -f agent` (or without `-f` when `follow=False`).\n   - `status_payload` → same remote `megaplan status` invocation as Railway, using `ssh_exec`.\n   - `down` → `docker compose -p <proj> down` (keeps volume).\n   - `destroy` → `docker compose -p <proj> down -v`.\n   - `upload_file` → `docker compose cp <src> agent:<dest>`.\n   - `read_remote_file` → `docker compose exec -T agent cat <path>`.\n3. **Guard** provider construction: if `docker` is not on PATH, raise `CliError(\"provider_unavailable\", \"docker CLI not found — install: https://docs.docker.com/get-docker/\")`. Mirrors the Railway pattern (`railway.py:27-29`).\n4. **Materialization:** extend `materialize_deploy_dir` in `megaplan/cloud/template.py:157` to emit `docker-compose.yaml` when `spec.provider == \"local\"` (new helper `_render_resource_template` call already exists).\n\n### Step 8: SSH provider (`megaplan/cloud/providers/ssh.py`)\n**Scope:** Medium\n1. **Create** `megaplan/cloud/providers/ssh.py::SshProvider`:\n   - Construction requires `ssh` on PATH (reuse the `shutil.which` + `CliError` pattern). Resolves identity/user/host/port from `SshSpec`.\n   - `_remote_run(cmd: str)` runs `ssh [-i identity] -p port user@host -- bash -lc '<cmd>'`.\n   - `build` → rsync deploy dir to `<remote_dir>`, then `_remote_run(\"cd <remote_dir> && docker build -t megaplan-cloud-<proj> .\")`. Requires `rsync` on both sides; if missing locally, fall back to `scp -r` with an info-level warning.\n   - `deploy` → write env file remotely, then `docker run -d --name <container> --env-file ... -v <vol>:/workspace -p <port>:<port> megaplan-cloud-<proj>`.\n   - `ssh_exec` → `_remote_run(\"docker exec <container> bash -lc '<cmd>'\")`.\n   - `attach` → `ssh ... -t docker exec -it <container> tmux attach -t agent`.\n   - `logs` → `ssh ... docker logs <-f> <container>`.\n   - `status_payload`, `down`, `destroy`, `upload_file`, `read_remote_file` follow the Railway pattern over `_remote_run`.\n2. **Quoting safety:** use `shlex.quote` on every user-provided string that's interpolated into the remote command line (paths, env file names, container name). This mirrors the existing pattern in `cloud/cli.py:153`.\n\n### Step 9: Provider tests (`tests/test_cloud_local.py`, `tests/test_cloud_ssh.py`, `tests/test_cloud_local_lifecycle.py`)\n**Scope:** Medium\n1. **Unit tests (mocked subprocess)** — pattern from `tests/test_cloud_railway.py`:\n   - `tests/test_cloud_local.py` monkeypatches `subprocess.run` and `shutil.which`, exercises each `LocalProvider` method, asserts exact argv for `docker compose ...` commands and that the materialized `docker-compose.yaml` references the expected service/volume/port.\n   - `tests/test_cloud_ssh.py` similar for `SshProvider` — asserts each path produces the right `ssh user@host -- '<command>'` argv with `shlex.quote` applied.\n2. **Local docker-compose smoke test** — `tests/test_cloud_local_lifecycle.py`, `@pytest.mark.slow`, skipped if `docker compose` isn't reachable (same skip pattern as `tests/test_cloud_docker_build.py:19-32`). Steps: materialize deploy dir for a minimal `mode: idle` spec, `compose build`, `compose up -d`, `exec agent which megaplan`, `compose down -v`. One lifecycle cycle; under 5 minutes.\n\n### Step 10: Toolchain extensibility in `cloud.yaml` (`megaplan/cloud/spec.py`, `megaplan/cloud/template.py`, `megaplan/cloud/templates/Dockerfile`)\n**Scope:** Medium\n1. **Spec:** add `ToolchainSpec` dataclass and parse a top-level `toolchains:` list in `load_spec`. Each entry is either:\n   - a string alias — `rust`, `go`, or `java` — resolved to a built-in install recipe; or\n   - a mapping — `{name: <custom-label>, install: \"<shell snippet>\"}` where `install` is a multi-line shell script run as a `RUN` layer.\n   Validate that every alias is known and every custom entry has non-empty `install`. Default: empty list (baseline Python+Node image).\n2. **Template:** convert `megaplan/cloud/templates/Dockerfile` into a templated string keyed by a new `TOOLCHAIN_BLOCK` placeholder. Render the block in `template.py` by looking up built-in recipes (kept in a `_TOOLCHAIN_RECIPES` dict in `template.py`) and concatenating user snippets as additional `RUN` stages inserted after the Node install and before `COPY entrypoint.sh`. Each recipe also updates `ENV PATH` if needed (e.g. `/root/.cargo/bin` for rust, `/usr/local/go/bin` for go).\n3. **Docs:** update `megaplan/cloud/templates/cloud.yaml.tmpl` with a commented `toolchains:` example showing one alias and one custom entry.\n4. **Tests:** extend `tests/test_cloud_template.py`:\n   - `test_toolchain_rust_renders_cargo_install`\n   - `test_toolchain_custom_snippet_renders_verbatim`\n   - `test_toolchain_unknown_alias_rejected`\n   - `test_no_toolchain_block_leaves_dockerfile_unchanged` — preserves the \"no breaking change from v0.19.0\" constraint by diffing against the frozen baseline.\n\n---\n\n## Phase 3: Cloud wrappers + hardening + migration\n\n### Step 11: `megaplan cloud chain <spec.yaml>` and `cloud bootstrap <idea-file>` (`megaplan/cloud/cli.py`)\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **Extend** `build_cloud_parser` (`megaplan/cloud/cli.py:22`) with two new subparsers:\n   - `chain` — positional `spec` (path on disk), optional `--remote-dir` (default derives from `spec.repo.workspace`).\n   - `bootstrap` — positional `idea_file`, required `--plan-name`, optional `--robustness` (default `\"standard\"`).\n2. **Dispatch in `run_cloud_cli`:**\n   - `cloud chain`: resolve the spec file locally, `provider.upload_file(local_path, f\"{workspace}/chain.yaml\")`, then `provider.ssh_exec(f\"cd {workspace} && megaplan chain start --spec {remote_path}\")`. Relay output, surface exit code.\n   - `cloud bootstrap`: upload idea file to `{workspace}/idea.txt`, then `provider.ssh_exec(f\"cd {workspace} && megaplan init --project-dir {workspace} --name {plan} --idea-file {idea_path} --auto-start --robustness {robustness}\")`. Relay output.\n   - Both commands **must** call existing core subcommands — no cloud-side parsing of chain state or plan state.\n\n### Step 12: `megaplan cloud status --chain` (`megaplan/cloud/cli.py`)\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **Add** `--chain` flag to the existing `status` subparser.\n2. **Implementation in `run_cloud_cli`:** when `--chain` is set, resolve the cloud spec's `chain.spec` (remote path) → compute the remote `chain_state.json` path with the same `_state_path_for` logic that `chain.py:205` uses. Fetch via `provider.read_remote_file(state_path)`, parse with `ChainState.from_dict(...)`, also fetch the chain spec YAML and parse with `chain.load_spec(...)`, then call `chain.format_chain_status(spec, state)` (imported from Step 2). Emit the same structured+pretty payload the local `chain status` prints.\n3. **Contract test** (`tests/test_cloud_chain_status.py`): mock `provider.read_remote_file` to return canned YAML + JSON; assert `cloud status --chain` prints a payload structurally identical to what local `chain status --spec` produces for the same inputs. This is the guardrail that enforces \"no cloud-specific chain status parser.\"\n\n### Step 13: Secret redaction in cloud logs/exec output (`megaplan/cloud/redact.py`, provider call sites)\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **Create** `megaplan/cloud/redact.py` with a single `redact(text: str, secret_names: list[str], env: Mapping[str, str] | None = None) -> str` function. Replaces:\n   - Literal known secret values drawn from `env` (default `os.environ`) for every name in `secret_names` — only when value length ≥ 8 to avoid over-matching.\n   - A compiled pattern matching `<NAME>=<value>` and `<NAME>: <value>` for each secret name, replacing the value with `***REDACTED***`.\n   - A default regex catching anything that looks like an obvious API key (e.g. `sk-[A-Za-z0-9]{20,}`, `ghp_[A-Za-z0-9]{30,}`).\n2. **Wire** redaction at the point output is relayed to the user. Touch points:\n   - `cloud/cli.py::_relay_output` (currently at `cli.py:227`) — redact `result.stdout` / `result.stderr` before writing.\n   - Provider `logs()` (non-follow case only — follow-mode streams directly through the child, no interception). Document the follow-mode gap in the helper's docstring.\n3. **Tests** (`tests/test_cloud_redact.py`): table-driven cases asserting literal values, `NAME=value` style, `NAME: value` style, and the `sk-...`/`ghp_...` default patterns all redact.\n\n### Step 14: Missing-CLI errors with documentation links (`megaplan/cloud/providers/{railway,local,ssh}.py`)\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **Standardize** the provider-unavailable messages to include both \"what's missing\" and \"where to install\":\n   - Railway: already uses `INSTALL_LINK` — leave intact, but wrap in a common formatter.\n   - Docker (Local + any usage inside Railway for `docker build`): `docker CLI not found. Install Docker: https://docs.docker.com/get-docker/`\n   - SSH: `ssh not on PATH`; `rsync not on PATH (using scp fallback — 10× slower)` — downgraded to a warning, not an error, via `sys.stderr.write`.\n2. **Ensure** all three errors come through as `CliError(\"provider_unavailable\", ...)` so the existing `_emit_error` serializer in `cloud/cli.py:234` produces a consistent JSON shape.\n\n### Step 15: Retire `~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev/` (reigh-migration.md in megaplan docs/, user-run steps)\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **Draft** a short runbook at `docs/cloud-migration-from-reigh.md` (this is the one doc this plan adds — needed because the folder is outside the megaplan repo and the user has to run the migration themselves). Steps:\n   - `megaplan cloud init --cloud-yaml ./cloud.yaml`\n   - Copy the env values from the current reigh `.env` into local env vars (or a `.env` committed with care — `.gitignore` already excludes it post-v0.19.0).\n   - `megaplan cloud build && megaplan cloud deploy`\n   - Run `megaplan cloud bootstrap ./idea.txt --plan-name reigh-parity-smoke` for a tiny verify plan.\n   - `megaplan cloud status` → expect `done`.\n   - Delete `~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev/` and replace with `~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev/MIGRATED.md` (single-line pointer to `megaplan cloud` and the runbook).\n2. **Deliverable from this plan:** the runbook markdown + the `cloud bootstrap` flow it depends on. The actual folder deletion is a human-executed step — flagged as `info` in success criteria since the reigh folder lives outside this repo's sandbox.\n\n---\n\n## Execution Order\n1. **Phase 1 first (Steps 1–4).** Core commands unlock everything downstream: Step 11 (`cloud chain`/`cloud bootstrap`) and Step 12 (`cloud status --chain`) reuse `chain.format_chain_status` and `init --idea-file` as-is. Land + tested before any cloud wrapper work.\n2. **Phase 2 provider plumbing (Steps 5–6)** before the concrete providers (Steps 7–8): unlocking the spec and expanding the base class are cheap, and getting them right prevents rework on the two concrete providers.\n3. **Concrete providers (Steps 7–8) + tests (Step 9)** can proceed in parallel for local and ssh — they share no code.\n4. **Toolchain block (Step 10)** is independent of providers; can be landed any time after Phase 1 but needs to ship before the reigh migration so the reigh cloud.yaml can specify the same toolchains reigh currently uses.\n5. **Phase 3 (Steps 11–14)** after Phases 1–2 are green.\n6. **Reigh retirement (Step 15)** last — requires every earlier deliverable to be landed.\n\n## Validation Order\n1. Unit tests for every new/changed core module first (`pytest tests/test_chain.py tests/test_handle_init_idea_file.py tests/test_core_without_cloud.py -x`) — cheapest and catches the core/cloud isolation contract immediately.\n2. Cloud unit tests (`pytest tests/test_cloud_spec.py tests/test_cloud_local.py tests/test_cloud_ssh.py tests/test_cloud_template.py tests/test_cloud_redact.py -x`) — all mocked-subprocess, fast.\n3. Full test suite (`pytest`) — ensure no regression in pre-existing cloud / chain / handler tests.\n4. Local docker-compose smoke test (`pytest -m slow tests/test_cloud_local_lifecycle.py`) — run once pre-merge on a host with docker.\n5. Manual end-to-end: deploy a `provider: local` spec with `mode: idle`, run `cloud bootstrap` against a trivial idea file, watch it go → done via `cloud status`. Then `cloud destroy`.\n6. Reigh migration is a human-run step — use the runbook (Step 15) to verify parity (a tiny plan completes end-to-end against the new in-tree deployment with the same env as reigh's).\n",

    "Toolchain entry format — is a mixed list of either strings (built-in aliases like `rust`/`go`/`java`) OR mappings (`{name, install}` for custom) acceptable, or do you prefer a single uniform shape (e.g. always `{name, install?}`)? The uniform shape is more verbose but easier to validate.",
    "`megaplan init --auto-start` — run `auto.drive()` in-process and block the CLI (simple, matches `chain`'s model) or fire-and-forget via tmux like the cloud entrypoint does (so `cloud bootstrap` returns quickly)? I've assumed in-process/blocking; `cloud bootstrap` then streams output until terminal.",
    "Local provider shape — single-container `docker compose` with one `agent` service bind-mounted to a host workspace dir, OR a multi-container compose file that mirrors the Railway shape (agent + separate healthcheck)? I've assumed single-container; the healthserver.py already runs inside the agent image.",
    "Reigh retirement — should the plan deliverable include a script or command that actually deletes `~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev/` and writes `MIGRATED.md`, or just a runbook that the user executes manually? The folder is outside this repo's sandbox so automated deletion requires out-of-working-dir permission; I've assumed manual.",
    "Secret redaction scope — redact only on the non-streaming paths (capture-then-redact-then-emit), or also wrap the follow-mode `logs -f` stream with a line-buffered redactor? The follow-mode redactor is more invasive (needs stream parsing); I've assumed non-streaming only, with a docstring caveat."

  "assumptions": [
    "`megaplan chain start --spec PATH` is a named subcommand under the existing `chain` parser and drives through the same `run_chain(...)` path the bare `megaplan chain --spec PATH` uses today; the bare form stays as a backcompat alias.",
    "`format_chain_status(spec, state)` lives in `megaplan.chain` (core) and is reused verbatim by `cloud status --chain` — this is what the brief means by 'do NOT reinvent the parser.'",
    "`--auto-start` on `megaplan init` runs `megaplan.auto.drive()` in-process and blocks until a terminal outcome; the init response payload embeds the driver outcome as `auto_outcome`.",
    "`toolchains:` accepts a mixed list: string aliases for built-in recipes (`rust`, `go`, `java`) or mappings `{name, install}` for custom install snippets. Empty list (default) preserves the v0.19.0 Python+Node image byte-for-byte.",
    "Local provider uses single-container `docker compose` with a bind-mounted `./workspace` host dir for the repo and a `.env` file for secrets, mirroring the Railway volume semantics as closely as possible.",
    "SSH provider uses plain `ssh`+`docker` over a bash login shell — rsync for deploy-dir upload with a `scp -r` fallback; no orchestration beyond a single named container.",
    "Fly provider is explicitly skipped per the 'OPTIONAL' note; `provider: fly` still errors with a dedicated 'future provider' message so users get a clear signal.",
    "Secret redaction is applied in the capture-then-emit paths (`_relay_output`, `logs --no-follow`, `status` response). Follow-mode `logs -f` is not intercepted; this limitation is documented in the redactor's docstring.",
    "Reigh retirement is documented as a runbook (new `docs/cloud-migration-from-reigh.md`); the actual `rm -rf` and `MIGRATED.md` write happens outside this repo and is a human-executed step.",
    "No version bump or CHANGELOG entry lands in this plan — sprint 2 ships as v0.20.0 but the bump + changelog commit is an explicit separate follow-up, per the brief's non-goals."
  ],

        Idea:
Sprint 2 of the megaplan cloud feature — extend core megaplan ergonomics, add local + ssh providers, ship toolchain extensibility in cloud.yaml, retire the old reigh-megaplan-dev folder.

### Context
Sprint 1 shipped in v0.19.0 on 2026-04-21: built-in megaplan cloud subcommand (init/build/deploy/status/attach/logs/exec/resume/down/destroy) with a Railway provider, extracted from the ad-hoc setup at ~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev/. Spec schema lives in megaplan/cloud/spec.py, templates in megaplan/cloud/templates/, providers in megaplan/cloud/providers/.

Architectural principle established in sprint 1 and now codified: CORE megaplan owns capabilities, CLOUD subcommands are thin transport wrappers. Do not reinvent chain state parsing in cloud. Do not grow a parallel 'cloud chain *' surface. Core gets the command; cloud wraps it for remote execution.

### Sprint 2 deliverables

Core megaplan additions (must work locally without any cloud dependency):
1. megaplan chain start --spec <path> — validate spec, resolve referenced idea files, kick auto-loop per milestone. Replaces the 'init + auto' pair for chain runs.
2. megaplan chain status --spec <path> — read chain_state.json, pretty-print current_milestone, completed, remaining, per-milestone status. Unit tests against synthetic chain states.
3. megaplan init --idea-file <path> with optional --auto-start flag — read idea from a file instead of the positional CLI arg.

Cloud additions (thin wrappers over the new core commands):
4. megaplan cloud chain <spec.yaml> — upload spec, then cloud exec to call megaplan chain start remotely.
5. megaplan cloud bootstrap <idea-file> — upload idea file, then cloud exec to call megaplan init --idea-file --auto-start remotely.
6. megaplan cloud status --chain — fetch remote chain_state.json, invoke the core chain-status formatter on it. Do NOT reinvent the parser.

New providers:
7. Local provider (docker compose) — fastest iteration target, no Railway/cloud dependency. Becomes the CI-friendly smoke-test target.
8. SSH provider (plain docker-over-ssh) — 'any host with docker + ssh access' story.

Toolchain extensibility:
9. toolchains: block in cloud.yaml — declares one or more toolchains (rust, go, java, or custom with user-provided install snippet). Template renderer appends corresponding Dockerfile stages. Without this, the image is Python/Node only.

Hardening + retirement:
10. Secret redaction in cloud logs output (match known secret name patterns, replace with ***REDACTED***).
11. Clear error messages + documentation link when railway or docker CLI is missing at runtime.
12. ~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev/ migration — deploy cloud with a cloud.yaml matching reigh's current env, run a tiny plan to verify parity, then delete the folder and replace with a MIGRATED.md pointer.

### Hard constraints
- Core commands MUST work locally without any cloud code loaded.
- Cloud wrappers MUST call out to core commands, not reimplement.
- Fly provider is OPTIONAL (skip if time-pressured).
- Preserve v0.19.0's public surface; this is additive.
- No breaking cloud.yaml changes from v0.19.0 (add fields, don't rename).
- Unit tests for every new core command; mocked-subprocess tests for every new provider; smoke test for local provider docker-compose lifecycle.

### Explicit non-goals
- No Kubernetes provider.
- No chain-of-chains.
- No cloud-specific chain status parser (the whole point of the core-first architecture).
- No version bump in this plan — sprint 2 ships as v0.20.0 but the bump + CHANGELOG commit is a separate follow-up.

- Sprint 1 landed in v0.19.0 (2026-04-21). Full CHANGELOG entry in CHANGELOG.md.
- Reigh parity reference: ~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev/ and megaplan/cloud/_reference/ (gitignored local copy of same).
- Verified launcher: ./.venv/bin/python -m megaplan (system python is 3.8, fails on NotRequired).
- Sprint 1 target retrospective: cloud subcommand CLI is already wired via megaplan/cloud/cli.py and dispatched from megaplan/cli.py.

Robustness: standard — feature-rich with architecture decisions to enforce, multiple independent pieces worth independent critique, gate useful for scope discipline.
