## Phase 2: Cloud infrastructure refactors + providers

### Step 7: Persistent per-project deploy-dir state (`megaplan/cloud/cli.py`) — resolves FLAG-001, all_locations-1
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Introduce** `_persistent_deploy_dir(spec: CloudSpec) -> Path` in `megaplan/cloud/cli.py`:
   - Railway: still returns a `TemporaryDirectory` context (stateless; the CLI pushes via `railway up`).
   - Local: returns `~/.megaplan/cloud/<compose_project>/` (mkdir -p), **not** ephemeral. `docker-compose.yaml`, `.env`, and `workspace/` persist across `build → deploy → exec → logs → down → destroy`. `destroy` clears the dir only after `docker compose down -v` succeeds.
   - SSH: returns `~/.megaplan/cloud/ssh-<host>/` for the same reason (rsync source of truth + `.env` tracking).
2. **Refactor** `run_cloud_cli` (`megaplan/cloud/cli.py:103-168`): `_materialized_deploy_dir` becomes provider-aware — railway still yields from a temp dir context; local/ssh yield the persistent path after materialization. `destroy` is the only command that clears the persistent dir (after down succeeds).
3. **Dispatch change:** every cloud action other than `init` / `exec` / `attach` / `logs` / `status` / `resume` / `down` / `destroy` that currently rebuilds from `_materialized_deploy_dir` now works from the stable dir for local/ssh, enabling compose-style `exec`/`logs`/`down`/`destroy` to find `docker-compose.yaml` on every call.
4. **Test** (`tests/test_cloud_persistent_dir.py`): mock `shutil.which` + `subprocess.run`; call `build`, then `exec`, then `down` against a `provider: local` spec; assert the same materialized deploy dir is reused across all three.

### Step 8: Generalize session/attach glue (`megaplan/cloud/cli.py`) — resolves all_locations-2
**Scope:** Small
1. **Rework** the `--session` flag at `megaplan/cloud/cli.py:57-62` and the `_provider_for_action` override at `182-188` so `--session` is only accepted when the active provider advertises it:
   - Add a `Provider.supports_session: bool` class attribute (default `False`; Railway override to `True`; Local/SSH leave default).
   - In `run_cloud_cli`, if `getattr(args, "session", None)` is set but `provider.supports_session` is `False`, raise `CliError("invalid_args", "--session is only supported for provider: railway")`.
2. **Rationale-comment** at the flag's argparse site pointing at `Provider.supports_session` so the next provider addition knows where to opt in.
3. **Test** (`tests/test_cloud_cli_session.py`): assert that `cloud attach --session X` against a `provider: local` spec raises `CliError("invalid_args", ...)`.

### Step 9: Unlock `local` and `ssh` providers in the spec loader (`megaplan/cloud/spec.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Change** `load_spec` (`megaplan/cloud/spec.py:167-172`): accept `"railway" | "local" | "ssh"`. Keep `"fly"` in a dedicated `FUTURE_PROVIDERS = ("fly",)` tuple with a distinct `invalid_spec` error ("Fly provider is deferred to a future sprint; use railway, local, or ssh for now."). Remove `SPRINT_TWO_PROVIDERS`.
2. **Add** `LocalSpec(compose_project: str = "megaplan-cloud", workdir: str | None = None)` and `SshSpec(host: str, user: str = "root", port: int = 22, identity_file: str | None = None, remote_dir: str = "/var/lib/megaplan-cloud", container: str = "megaplan-agent")` dataclasses. Parse in `load_spec` when the provider matches.
3. **Add** `local: LocalSpec | None = None`, `ssh: SshSpec | None = None` to `CloudSpec` (kwargs, field order unchanged; all existing `CloudSpec(...)` test constructions continue to work).
4. **Update** `tests/test_cloud_spec.py`: drop the reject-sprint-2-providers test; add happy-path tests for `provider: local` / `provider: ssh`; add a `provider: fly` test asserting the distinct future-provider error.

### Step 10: `Provider` base + registry + file-transfer hooks (`megaplan/cloud/providers/base.py`, `providers/railway.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** optional hooks on `Provider`:
   - `upload_file(self, src: Path, dest: str) -> None` — used by `cloud chain` / `cloud bootstrap`. Base raises `CliError("not_implemented", ...)`; Railway + Local + SSH override.
   - `read_remote_file(self, path: str) -> str` — used by `cloud status --chain`. Same pattern.
   - `supports_session: bool = False` (Step 8).
2. **Replace** the `if name != "railway"` hardcoded guard (`base.py:52-54`) with a lazy name→factory dict: `{"railway": ..., "local": ..., "ssh": ...}`. Unknown names raise `CliError("invalid_spec", f"Unknown cloud provider '{name}'. Known: railway, local, ssh")`.
3. **Implement** `upload_file` / `read_remote_file` on `RailwayProvider` via `railway ssh --service ... -- "base64 -d > dest"` (base64 in over stdin for safety) and `railway ssh -- cat <path>` respectively. Base64 avoids shell-quoting pitfalls on binary-ish content.

### Step 11: Local provider (`megaplan/cloud/providers/local.py`, `megaplan/cloud/templates/docker-compose.yaml.tmpl`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Create** `megaplan/cloud/templates/docker-compose.yaml.tmpl` — single `agent` service built from the materialized `Dockerfile`, bind-mounting `./workspace` (inside the **persistent** deploy dir from Step 7), env via `.env`, port exposed per `resources.port`.
2. **Create** `megaplan/cloud/providers/local.py::LocalProvider` implementing the full `Provider` ABC. **All methods operate on the persistent deploy dir** (from Step 7) — not a fresh temp each time:
   - `build` → `docker compose -p <proj> -f <deploy>/docker-compose.yaml build`
   - `deploy` → write `<deploy>/.env` from secrets, `docker compose ... up -d`
   - `ssh_exec` → `docker compose ... exec -T agent bash -lc '<command>'`
   - `attach` → `docker compose ... exec agent tmux attach -t agent` (bash -l fallback)
   - `logs` → `docker compose ... logs [-f] agent` (see Step 17 for follow-mode wrapping)
   - `status_payload` → same remote `megaplan status` via `ssh_exec`
   - `down` → `docker compose ... down` (keeps volume)
   - `destroy` → `docker compose ... down -v`, then clear the persistent dir
   - `upload_file` → `docker compose ... cp <src> agent:<dest>`
   - `read_remote_file` → `docker compose ... exec -T agent cat <path>`
3. **Guard** construction: if `docker` not on PATH, raise `CliError("provider_unavailable", "docker CLI not found. Install Docker: https://docs.docker.com/get-docker/")`. Step 18 standardizes.
4. **Materialize** `docker-compose.yaml` in `megaplan/cloud/template.py` when `spec.provider == "local"`.

### Step 12: SSH provider (`megaplan/cloud/providers/ssh.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Create** `SshProvider`:
   - Construct requires `ssh` on PATH. Reads `host`/`user`/`port`/`identity_file`/`remote_dir`/`container` from `SshSpec`.
   - `_remote_run(cmd)` → `ssh [-i identity] -p port user@host -- bash -lc '<shlex.quote(cmd)>'`.
   - `build` → rsync persistent-deploy-dir contents to `remote_dir`, then `_remote_run("cd <remote_dir> && docker build -t megaplan-cloud-<container> .")`. `scp -r` fallback if rsync absent (warn, not fail).
   - `deploy` → write `.env` remotely, `docker run -d --name <container> --env-file <remote>/.env -v <workspace_vol>:/workspace -p <port>:<port> megaplan-cloud-<container>`.
   - `ssh_exec` → `_remote_run("docker exec <container> bash -lc '<cmd>'")`.
   - `attach` → `ssh -t ... docker exec -it <container> tmux attach -t agent`.
   - `logs` → `ssh ... docker logs <-f> <container>` (follow-mode → Step 17 wrapper).
   - `status_payload` / `down` / `destroy` / `upload_file` / `read_remote_file` follow the Railway pattern over `_remote_run`.
2. **Quoting safety:** `shlex.quote` every interpolated user-provided string. Explicit pattern from `megaplan/cloud/cli.py:153`.

### Step 13: Provider tests (`tests/test_cloud_local.py`, `tests/test_cloud_ssh.py`, `tests/test_cloud_local_lifecycle.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Unit tests (mocked subprocess)** following `tests/test_cloud_railway.py`:
   - `tests/test_cloud_local.py` — exact `docker compose -p <proj> ...` argv for every ABC method; materialized `docker-compose.yaml` structure check; construction fails with expected `CliError` if `docker` is absent.
   - `tests/test_cloud_ssh.py` — exact `ssh user@host -- 'docker ...'` argv for every method with `shlex.quote` applied to interpolated paths; construction fails if `ssh` absent.
2. **Smoke test** — `tests/test_cloud_local_lifecycle.py`, `@pytest.mark.slow`, skip pattern from `tests/test_cloud_docker_build.py:19-32`. One cycle: materialize → `build` → `deploy` → `ssh_exec("which megaplan")` → `destroy`. Under 5 minutes. Uses the persistent deploy dir from Step 7.

### Step 14: Toolchain extensibility (`megaplan/cloud/spec.py`, `megaplan/cloud/template.py`, `megaplan/cloud/templates/Dockerfile`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Spec:** add `ToolchainSpec` dataclass. `toolchains:` entries are either:
   - A string alias — `rust` | `go` | `java` — resolved to a built-in install recipe, or
   - A mapping `{name: <label>, install: "<shell snippet>"}` for custom snippets.
   Validate alias membership and non-empty `install`. Default: empty list.
2. **Template:** introduce `TOOLCHAIN_BLOCK` placeholder in `megaplan/cloud/templates/Dockerfile` between the Node install and `COPY entrypoint.sh`. Render via a `_TOOLCHAIN_RECIPES` dict in `template.py`; each recipe contributes a `RUN` stage and optional `ENV PATH` additions (e.g. `/root/.cargo/bin`).
3. **Docs:** commented `toolchains:` example in `megaplan/cloud/templates/cloud.yaml.tmpl`.
4. **Tests** in `tests/test_cloud_template.py`:
   - `test_toolchain_rust_renders_cargo_install`
   - `test_toolchain_custom_snippet_renders_verbatim`
   - `test_toolchain_unknown_alias_rejected`
   - `test_no_toolchain_block_leaves_dockerfile_unchanged` — byte-level diff against the v0.19.0 baseline (no-break invariant).

---

## Phase 3: Cloud wrappers + hardening + migration

### Step 15: `megaplan cloud chain` + `cloud bootstrap` (`megaplan/cloud/cli.py`) — resolves FLAG-002, correctness-2, issue_hints-1
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** subparsers under `cloud_sub`:
   - `chain` — positional `spec` (local path).
   - `bootstrap` — positional `idea_file` (local path), **optional** `--plan-name` (delegates to core slug default when omitted — issue_hints-1), optional `--robustness` (default `standard`).
2. **`cloud chain` full input staging (FLAG-002, correctness-2):**
   - Parse the spec locally via `megaplan.chain.load_spec(spec_path)` (core-owned — no new parser in cloud).
   - Enumerate every file the remote `megaplan chain start` will open: the spec itself, plus every `milestone.idea` path.
   - For each file, compute a remote path that preserves the structure the spec expects. If the spec's `milestones[*].idea` uses absolute paths (e.g. `/workspace/ideas/m1.txt`), upload to exactly that remote path. If relative, upload under `{workspace}/` preserving relative layout.
   - `provider.upload_file(local_file, remote_path)` per file; finally `provider.ssh_exec(f"cd {shlex.quote(workspace)} && megaplan chain start --spec {shlex.quote(remote_spec)}")`.
   - If a referenced idea file doesn't exist locally, fail fast with `CliError("missing_idea_file", f"milestone 'X' idea not found locally: <path>")` — mirrors the remote validator so the error surfaces before any upload.
3. **`cloud bootstrap`:**
   - Upload idea file to `{workspace}/idea.txt`.
   - Build a remote command, where `--plan-name` is passed through only when the user provided it; otherwise it's omitted and core's slug default applies:
     ```python
     remote = f"cd {wx} && megaplan init --project-dir {wx} --idea-file {ideaq} --auto-start --robustness {rq}"
     if args.plan_name:
         remote += f" --name {shlex.quote(args.plan_name)}"
     ```
   - `provider.ssh_exec(remote)`; relay output.
4. **Cloud-side isolation:** neither wrapper parses plan state or chain state on its own. `chain.load_spec` is used for the enumeration pass only — it returns the parsed `ChainSpec` via core code.

### Step 16: `megaplan cloud status --chain` (`megaplan/cloud/cli.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `--chain` flag to the existing `cloud status` subparser.
2. **Implementation:** compute remote chain_state path via `chain._state_path_for(Path(spec.chain.spec))` (core helper, `megaplan/chain.py:205`). `provider.read_remote_file(state_path)` → `ChainState.from_dict(json.loads(...))`. `provider.read_remote_file(spec.chain.spec)` → `chain.load_spec(...)` (write the YAML to a tempfile locally because `load_spec` takes a `Path`). Then `chain.format_chain_status(...)` — the same function `chain status` calls locally. Emit structured + pretty payload.
3. **Contract test** (`tests/test_cloud_chain_status.py`): mock `provider.read_remote_file` to return canned YAML + JSON; assert the rendered payload is **structurally identical** to local `chain status --spec <same inputs>`. Guardrail that enforces "no cloud-specific chain status parser."

### Step 17: Secret redaction incl. follow-mode streaming (`megaplan/cloud/redact.py`, `megaplan/cloud/providers/*`) — resolves FLAG-004, issue_hints-2
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Create** `megaplan/cloud/redact.py`:
   - `redact(text, secret_names, env=None)` — replaces (a) literal secret values from `env` for each name when `len(value) >= 8`, (b) `NAME=value` and `NAME: value` patterns, (c) default API-key regexes (`sk-[A-Za-z0-9]{20,}`, `ghp_[A-Za-z0-9]{30,}`, `xoxb-...`) with `***REDACTED***`.
   - `stream_redact(proc: subprocess.Popen, secret_names, env=None)` — a **line-buffered** wrapper: iterates `proc.stdout` line by line, pipes each line through `redact`, writes the result to `sys.stdout`. Handles short-read tails by flushing on EOF. Same for `stderr` when available.
2. **Wire** redaction at every output relay path (closes FLAG-004 + issue_hints-2):
   - `megaplan/cloud/cli.py::_relay_output` (currently `cli.py:227`) — redact `result.stdout` / `result.stderr` before writing.
   - Provider `logs(follow=False)` — capture_output then redact.
   - Provider `logs(follow=True)` — **spawn via `subprocess.Popen` with `stdout=subprocess.PIPE, stderr=subprocess.PIPE, bufsize=1, text=True`**, then pass both pipes to `stream_redact`. This is the default `megaplan cloud logs` path and must not leak secrets.
   - Same Popen-plus-`stream_redact` pattern for `attach` (interactive session → Popen pty pipe is overkill; attach intentionally forks the ssh client for interactive use, so we document this gap in attach's docstring — users know interactive sessions show whatever the remote terminal emits).
3. **Refactor** `RailwayProvider.logs` / `LocalProvider.logs` / `SshProvider.logs` to factor a common `_logs_follow(argv)` helper that sets up the Popen + `stream_redact` pipeline and returns the exit code.
4. **Tests** (`tests/test_cloud_redact.py`):
   - Table-driven `redact`: literals, `NAME=value`, `NAME: value`, `sk-.../ghp_.../xoxb-...`.
   - `stream_redact` with a synthetic `Popen`-shaped mock whose `stdout` yields canned lines containing secrets; asserts every redaction hits and line ordering is preserved.
   - `tests/test_cloud_logs_follow_redacted.py`: monkeypatch `subprocess.Popen` in each provider's `logs` path; feed synthetic lines with secrets; assert `sys.stdout` output is redacted.

### Step 18: Missing-CLI errors with install links (`megaplan/cloud/providers/{railway,local,ssh}.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Common formatter** in `megaplan/cloud/providers/base.py::_missing_cli_error(binary, install_url)`:
   ```python
   raise CliError("provider_unavailable", f"{binary} CLI not found. Install: {install_url}")
   ```
2. **Wire** in each provider constructor:
   - Railway: `railway` → `https://docs.railway.app/develop/cli`
   - Local: `docker` → `https://docs.docker.com/get-docker/`
   - SSH: `ssh` → "already bundled with most systems; on Windows install OpenSSH via Optional Features"
3. **Test** (`tests/test_cloud_provider_unavailable.py`): monkeypatch `shutil.which` to return None for each binary; assert `CliError("provider_unavailable", ...)` with both a "what's missing" phrase and the URL/hint.

### Step 19: Retire `~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev/` (`docs/cloud-migration-from-reigh.md`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Draft** `docs/cloud-migration-from-reigh.md`:
   - `./.venv/bin/python -m megaplan cloud init --cloud-yaml ./cloud.yaml` — note the launcher explicitly.
   - Copy reigh `.env` into local env vars (or a `.gitignore`-covered `.env`).
   - `megaplan cloud build && megaplan cloud deploy`.
   - `megaplan cloud bootstrap ./idea.txt` (no `--plan-name` — core slug default) for a tiny parity plan.
   - `megaplan cloud status` → expect `done`.
   - Manual cleanup: `rm -rf ~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev && echo "Migrated to megaplan cloud. See cloud-migration-from-reigh.md." > ~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev/MIGRATED.md`.
2. **Deliverable from this plan** = the runbook + the `cloud bootstrap` flow it relies on. Folder deletion is out-of-repo and `info`-priority; no automation.

---

## Execution Order
1. **Phase 1 first (Steps 1–6).** Core commands + parser isolation + launcher fix unlock downstream. Step 4 (lazy cloud subparser) and Step 5 (sys.executable -m megaplan) must land before Step 6's isolation test can pass.
2. **Phase 2 foundations (Steps 7, 8, 9, 10)** before concrete providers (11, 12): the persistent deploy dir (7) and session-glue generalization (8) are prerequisites for Local and SSH. Spec unlock (9) and base registry (10) are cheap.
3. **Concrete providers (Steps 11, 12) + tests (13)** can land in parallel — they share only `Provider` base.
4. **Toolchain (Step 14)** is independent; can land any time post-Phase 1.
5. **Phase 3 (Steps 15–18)** after Phase 2 is green. Step 17 (redaction) touches every provider's `logs` — land after providers exist.
6. **Step 19 (reigh retirement)** last.

## Validation Order
1. Core unit tests — cheap, proves core/cloud isolation contract: `pytest tests/test_chain.py tests/test_handle_init_idea_file.py tests/test_core_without_cloud.py -x`.
2. Launcher-path regression: `pytest tests/test_auto.py tests/test_chain.py -x` — asserts updated argv.
3. Cloud unit tests: `pytest tests/test_cloud_spec.py tests/test_cloud_persistent_dir.py tests/test_cloud_cli_session.py tests/test_cloud_local.py tests/test_cloud_ssh.py tests/test_cloud_template.py tests/test_cloud_redact.py tests/test_cloud_logs_follow_redacted.py tests/test_cloud_provider_unavailable.py tests/test_cloud_chain_status.py -x`.
4. Full suite (`pytest`) — no regression in existing cloud/chain/handler tests; verifies the v0.19.0 `cloud.yaml` public surface is intact.
5. `@pytest.mark.slow` smoke: `pytest -m slow tests/test_cloud_local_lifecycle.py` on a host with docker.
6. Manual end-to-end: `provider: local` + `mode: idle` + `cloud bootstrap ./idea.txt` (no `--plan-name`) → done via `cloud status` → `cloud destroy`.
7. Reigh migration — human-run per Step 19 runbook.

  "recommendation": "ITERATE",
  "rationale": "Iteration 2 made genuine progress \u2014 4 blocking flags resolved (FLAG-001, FLAG-004, all_locations-1, all_locations-2) with concrete structural fixes (persistent deploy dir, stream_redact follow-mode, supports_session pattern). Score dropped 19\u219218, plan delta 71.3%. But 12 open flags remain, and they are concrete plan-quality issues with obvious fixes \u2014 not unresolvable architectural tensions, and no flag has been re-raised after being claimed fixed (addressed_then_reopened_count=0 across all 17 fuzzy groups). Not TIEBREAKER territory. The remaining issues cluster into five concrete revisions, each with a clear path forward: (1) **Parser mechanism bug (correctness-1/FLAG-005)** \u2014 Step 4's sketch reparses `argv[1:]` against `build_cloud_parser(subparsers)` which registers a top-level `cloud` command; the mechanism doesn't match the API. Fix: defer *dispatch* not parsing \u2014 keep the stub parser for core parse, but when `args.command == \"cloud\"`, re-run parsing against a fresh top-level parser that now includes the real cloud subparser, passing the full `argv` (not `argv[1:]`). Or refactor `build_cloud_parser` to take `argv` and return parsed args directly. (2) **Path staging strategy (FLAG-002/correctness-2)** \u2014 the bundled `chain.yaml.example` uses `/workspace/ideas/m1.txt` (remote paths). Step 15's \"exact-path upload\" local preflight would reject that shape. Fix: explicitly specify the mapping \u2014 accept two modes: absolute paths (treat as already-remote targets, upload local file if one exists at same path OR at a sibling `ideas/` dir relative to the spec, else error), relative paths (resolve relative to the spec file's dir on disk, upload under `{workspace}/` preserving relative layout). Document which shape the bundled example uses. (3) **Chain status transport for ad-hoc runs (FLAG-006/issue_hints-1/callers)** \u2014 `cloud status --chain` relies on `spec.chain.spec` which only exists when `cloud.yaml.mode == 'chain'`. Fix: when `cloud chain <spec>` is invoked, persist the remote spec path to `~/.megaplan/cloud/<proj>/last_chain.json` (local bookkeeping; cloud-side state isn't being mutated). `cloud status --chain` reads this marker, falling back to `spec.chain.spec` when present, and optionally accepts an explicit `--remote-spec <path>` override. (4) **Reigh runbook order bug (FLAG-007/issue_hints-2)** \u2014 trivial fix: write `MIGRATED.md` first, then `rm -rf ~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev/*` (glob to preserve parent dir), OR write the pointer inside the dir before deletion at a different location like `~/Documents/reigh-MIGRATED.md`. (5) **Docs scope (scope/all_locations)** \u2014 add `docs/cloud.md` to the must-update list; README line 108-113 points users there for the authoritative cloud reference. All five are plan-writing issues. None require human judgment or architectural redesign. The recurring debt subsystem overlap on input-provisioning (DEBT-047/049/051/052 from Sprint 1) is exactly what FLAG-002 surfaces \u2014 the fix is specifying the mapping strategy explicitly rather than deferring it implicitly again. The trajectory is acceptable for standard robustness at iteration 2: score down, no re-raises, 4/12 genuinely resolved. One more pass should close this out.",

  "settled_decisions": [
    {
      "id": "SD-001",
      "decision": "Core-first architecture: core owns capabilities (`chain start`, `chain status` formatter, `init --idea-file --auto-start`); cloud wrappers are thin transport calls to those core commands.",
      "rationale": "Established Sprint 1, reaffirmed in the brief, and the basis for the `no cloud-specific chain status parser` non-goal."
    },
    {
      "id": "SD-002",
      "decision": "Fly provider deferred; `provider: fly` produces a distinct 'future provider' error via a dedicated `FUTURE_PROVIDERS = (\"fly\",)` tuple, not a generic unknown-provider error.",
      "rationale": "Brief marks Fly explicitly optional; the distinct error avoids silent dead ends."
    },
    {
      "id": "SD-003",
      "decision": "No version bump or CHANGELOG entry in this plan; sprint 2 ships as v0.20.0 via a separate follow-up commit.",
      "rationale": "Explicit non-goal in the brief."
    },
    {
      "id": "SD-004",
      "decision": "`toolchains:` accepts a mixed list of string aliases (rust/go/java) and `{name, install}` mappings for custom snippets; empty list preserves the v0.19.0 Dockerfile byte-for-byte.",
      "rationale": "Expressive and additive; byte-identical default preserves no-break invariant."
    },
    {
      "id": "SD-005",
      "decision": "`megaplan init --auto-start` runs `auto.drive()` in-process, blocks until terminal, embeds `DriverOutcome` under an `auto_outcome` response key.",
      "rationale": "Matches chain's blocking model; cloud bootstrap streams remote stdout via `ssh_exec` for the same UX."
    },
    {
      "id": "SD-006",
      "decision": "Local and SSH providers use a persistent per-project deploy directory under `~/.megaplan/cloud/<compose_project>/`, not per-command TemporaryDirectory; Railway keeps TemporaryDirectory.",
      "rationale": "Resolved FLAG-001/all_locations-1 in iteration 2; needed for docker-compose lifecycle across build/exec/down."
    },
    {
      "id": "SD-007",
      "decision": "Follow-mode `logs -f` secret redaction uses a line-buffered `stream_redact(Popen)` wrapper; interactive `attach` is a documented gap.",
      "rationale": "Resolved FLAG-004 in iteration 2; attach's pty path is infeasible to line-wrap without breaking interactivity."
    },
    {
      "id": "SD-008",
      "decision": "`--session` flag gated via `Provider.supports_session: bool` class attribute (Railway True, Local/SSH default False); non-supporting providers raise `CliError(\"invalid_args\", ...)`.",
      "rationale": "Resolved all_locations-2 in iteration 2; replaces the Railway-hardcoded check with a provider-opt-in pattern."
    },
    {
      "id": "SD-009",
      "decision": "Lazy cloud subparser registration is directionally correct (SD from gate v1 warnings + iteration 2 acceptance) \u2014 parser isolation must be a code change, not just a test. Mechanism details are still being nailed down (see iteration 3 guidance).",
      "rationale": "The isolation test requirement can only pass if parser registration is lazy; all parties agree on the direction."
    },
    {
      "id": "SD-010",
      "decision": "Subprocess launchers in `auto.py` / `chain.py` use `[sys.executable, \"-m\", \"megaplan\", ...]`; in-container shell wrappers keep bare `megaplan` since the entrypoint installs it on PATH.",
      "rationale": "Resolved `callers` flag in iteration 2; matches the brief's verified launcher (`./.venv/bin/python -m megaplan`)."
    }
  ],
  "override_forced": false,
  "orchestrator_guidance": "Score improving (19.0 -> 18.0). Continue to revise. Verify unresolved flags against the plan and project code before accepting. Scope creep detected; compare the current plan against the original idea.",
  "robustness": "standard",
  "signals": {
    "iteration": 2,
    "idea": "Sprint 2 of the megaplan cloud feature \u2014 extend core megaplan ergonomics, add local + ssh providers, ship toolchain extensibility in cloud.yaml, retire the old reigh-megaplan-dev folder.\n\n### Context\nSprint 1 shipped in v0.19.0 on 2026-04-21: built-in megaplan cloud subcommand (init/build/deploy/status/attach/logs/exec/resume/down/destroy) with a Railway provider, extracted from the ad-hoc setup at ~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev/. Spec schema lives in megaplan/cloud/spec.py, templates in megaplan/cloud/templates/, providers in megaplan/cloud/providers/.\n\nArchitectural principle established in sprint 1 and now codified: CORE megaplan owns capabilities, CLOUD subcommands are thin transport wrappers. Do not reinvent chain state parsing in cloud. Do not grow a parallel 'cloud chain *' surface. Core gets the command; cloud wraps it for remote execution.\n\n### Sprint 2 deliverables\n\nCore megaplan additions (must work locally without any cloud dependency):\n1. megaplan chain start --spec <path> \u2014 validate spec, resolve referenced idea files, kick auto-loop per milestone. Replaces the 'init + auto' pair for chain runs.\n2. megaplan chain status --spec <path> \u2014 read chain_state.json, pretty-print current_milestone, completed, remaining, per-milestone status. Unit tests against synthetic chain states.\n3. megaplan init --idea-file <path> with optional --auto-start flag \u2014 read idea from a file instead of the positional CLI arg.\n\nCloud additions (thin wrappers over the new core commands):\n4. megaplan cloud chain <spec.yaml> \u2014 upload spec, then cloud exec to call megaplan chain start remotely.\n5. megaplan cloud bootstrap <idea-file> \u2014 upload idea file, then cloud exec to call megaplan init --idea-file --auto-start remotely.\n6. megaplan cloud status --chain \u2014 fetch remote chain_state.json, invoke the core chain-status formatter on it. Do NOT reinvent the parser.\n\nNew providers:\n7. Local provider (docker compose) \u2014 fastest iteration target, no Railway/cloud dependency. Becomes the CI-friendly smoke-test target.\n8. SSH provider (plain docker-over-ssh) \u2014 'any host with docker + ssh access' story.\n\nToolchain extensibility:\n9. toolchains: block in cloud.yaml \u2014 declares one or more toolchains (rust, go, java, or custom with user-provided install snippet). Template renderer appends corresponding Dockerfile stages. Without this, the image is Python/Node only.\n\nHardening + retirement:\n10. Secret redaction in cloud logs output (match known secret name patterns, replace with ***REDACTED***).\n11. Clear error messages + documentation link when railway or docker CLI is missing at runtime.\n12. ~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev/ migration \u2014 deploy cloud with a cloud.yaml matching reigh's current env, run a tiny plan to verify parity, then delete the folder and replace with a MIGRATED.md pointer.\n\n### Hard constraints\n- Core commands MUST work locally without any cloud code loaded.\n- Cloud wrappers MUST call out to core commands, not reimplement.\n- Fly provider is OPTIONAL (skip if time-pressured).\n- Preserve v0.19.0's public surface; this is additive.\n- No breaking cloud.yaml changes from v0.19.0 (add fields, don't rename).\n- Unit tests for every new core command; mocked-subprocess tests for every new provider; smoke test for local provider docker-compose lifecycle.\n\n### Explicit non-goals\n- No Kubernetes provider.\n- No chain-of-chains.\n- No cloud-specific chain status parser (the whole point of the core-first architecture).\n- No version bump in this plan \u2014 sprint 2 ships as v0.20.0 but the bump + CHANGELOG commit is a separate follow-up.\n\n### References\n- Sprint 1 landed in v0.19.0 (2026-04-21). Full CHANGELOG entry in CHANGELOG.md.\n- Reigh parity reference: ~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev/ and megaplan/cloud/_reference/ (gitignored local copy of same).\n- Verified launcher: ./.venv/bin/python -m megaplan (system python is 3.8, fails on NotRequired).\n- Sprint 1 target retrospective: cloud subcommand CLI is already wired via megaplan/cloud/cli.py and dispatched from megaplan/cli.py.\n\nRobustness: standard \u2014 feature-rich with architecture decisions to enforce, multiple independent pieces worth independent critique, gate useful for scope discipline.",
    "significant_flags": 12,

        "concern": "Cloud lifecycle: the proposed Local provider assumes a persistent compose project directory, but `run_cloud_cli()` currently materializes deploy assets in a per-command temporary directory and deletes them after each action.",
        "resolution": "Rev 2 addresses all 12 blocking flags from gate v1. Key structural additions: (a) Step 4 makes cloud subparser registration lazy in `megaplan/cli.py` so the isolation test can actually pass (FLAG-003, scope); (b) Step 5 standardizes `auto` / `chain` subprocess launchers to `sys.executable -m megaplan` matching the brief's verified launcher (callers); (c) Step 7 replaces per-command `TemporaryDirectory` with persistent `~/.megaplan/cloud/<proj>/` state so `docker compose` lifecycle works across build \u2192 exec \u2192 down (FLAG-001, all_locations-1); (d) Step 8 generalizes `--session` via `Provider.supports_session` rather than hardcoding Railway (all_locations-2); (e) Step 15 extends `cloud chain` to enumerate and upload every `milestones[*].idea` file the core chain runner will open, not just the top-level spec (FLAG-002, correctness-2); (f) Step 15 also makes `cloud bootstrap --plan-name` optional, delegating to core's slug default (issue_hints-1); (g) Step 3 explicitly resolves `idea_text` and replaces every `args.idea` reference at `handlers.py:769,777` (correctness-1); (h) Step 17 adds a `stream_redact` line-buffered wrapper and applies it to the default follow-mode `logs -f` path, not just captured output (FLAG-004, issue_hints-2). Success criteria updated with five new MUST items making each fix independently verifiable. No scope creep \u2014 every change is anchored to a blocking flag or to a constraint the brief stated but rev 1 missed."

        Plan template — simple format (adapt to the actual repo and scope):
````md
# Implementation Plan: [Title]

## Overview
Summarize the goal, current repository shape, and the constraints that matter.

## Main Phase

### Step 1: Audit the current behavior (`megaplan/prompts.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Inspect** the current implementation and call out the exact insertion points (`megaplan/prompts.py:29`).

### Step 2: Add the first change (`megaplan/evaluation.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Implement** the smallest viable change with exact file references (`megaplan/evaluation.py:1`).
2. **Capture** any tricky behavior with a short example.
   ```python
   issues = validate_plan_structure(plan_text)
   ```

### Step 3: Wire downstream behavior (`megaplan/handlers.py`, `megaplan/workers.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Update** the runtime flow in the touched files (`megaplan/handlers.py:400`, `megaplan/workers.py:199`).

### Step 4: Prove the change (`tests/test_evaluation.py`, `tests/test_megaplan.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Run** the cheapest targeted checks first (`tests/test_evaluation.py:1`).
2. **Finish** with broader verification once the wiring is in place (`tests/test_megaplan.py:1`).

## Execution Order
1. Update prompts and mocks before enforcing stricter validation.
2. Land higher-risk wiring after the validator and tests are ready.

## Validation Order
1. Start with focused unit tests.
2. Run the broader suite after the flow changes are in place.
````

For complex plans, use multiple phases:
````md
## Phase 1: Foundation — Dependencies, DB, Types

### Step 1: Install dependencies (`package.json`)
...

### Step 2: Create database migration (`supabase/migrations/`)
...

## Phase 2: Core Integration

### Step 3: Port the main component (`src/components/`)
...
````

Template guidance:
- Simple plans: use `## Main Phase` with `### Step N:` sections underneath.
- Complex plans: use multiple `## Phase N:` sections, each containing `### Step N:` steps. Step numbers are global (not per-phase).
- The flat `## Step N:` format (without phases) also works for backwards compatibility.
- Key invariants: one H1 title, one `## Overview`, numbered step sections (`### Step N:` or `## Step N:`), and at least one ordering section.

Root-cause check: no redirection needed. All 12 open flags are concrete, bounded fixes — the gate rationale explicitly says "not TIEBREAKER territory," "not architecturally blocked," and maps five clusters with obvious paths. Gate also warns about score-to-churn ratio (71% rewrite, 1-point improvement), so Rev 3 should be **surgical**, not another full rewrite. I'll touch only the five problem areas:

1. **Step 4 parser mechanism bug (FLAG-005, correctness-1, dispute of FLAG-003):** current sketch calls `build_cloud_parser(subparsers)` then reparses `argv[1:]` — API-inconsistent. Fix: split `build_cloud_parser` into (a) the existing under-subparsers shape, and (b) a new `_register_cloud_subcommands(parser)` helper that registers on *any* parser. Lazy path builds a fresh standalone cloud parser via the new helper.
2. **Step 15 path-staging contract (FLAG-002, correctness-2):** explicit local↔remote mapping — spec paths are **remote**, local sources come from `--idea-dir` (default = spec's dir), strip the `spec.repo.workspace` prefix, upload to exact remote path.
3. **Step 15+16 ad-hoc chain state transport (FLAG-006, issue_hints-1, callers):** persist `last_chain.json` marker locally after `cloud chain`; `cloud status --chain` reads marker → falls back to `spec.chain.spec` → accepts `--remote-spec` override.
4. **Step 19 runbook order (FLAG-007, issue_hints-2):** delete-then-write fails. Fix: write `MIGRATED.md`, then clear siblings with `find -mindepth 1 -delete`, or recreate dir after `rm -rf`.
5. **Docs scope (scope, all_locations):** add `docs/cloud.md` to the documentation surface as a MUST.

Tests: rust recipe, custom snippet, unknown alias rejection, and a byte-identical-baseline diff for the no-toolchain path.\n\n---\n\n## Phase 3: Cloud wrappers + hardening + migration\n\n### Step 15: `cloud chain` + `cloud bootstrap` with explicit path contract and marker persistence (`megaplan/cloud/cli.py`) — resolves FLAG-002, FLAG-006, correctness-2, issue_hints-1, callers\n**Scope:** Medium\n1. **Add** subparsers:\n   - `chain` — positional `spec` (local path), optional `--idea-dir <local>` (default: spec's parent directory).\n   - `bootstrap` — positional `idea_file` (local path), optional `--plan-name` (omit → core slug default), optional `--robustness` (default `standard`).\n2. **`cloud chain` explicit path contract (FLAG-002, correctness-2):**\n   - Parse the spec locally with `megaplan.chain.load_spec(spec_path)` — **enumeration only**; no reimplementation.\n   - **Spec paths are treated as remote paths** (matches `megaplan/cloud/templates/chain.yaml.example` + `docs/cloud.md`). The bundled example uses `/workspace/ideas/m1.txt` style, and that remains canonical.\n   - For each milestone, compute the **local source path** by:\n     - If the remote path begins with `spec.repo.workspace + \"/\"`, strip that prefix → relative tail (e.g. `/workspace/ideas/m1.txt` with `workspace: /workspace` → `ideas/m1.txt`).\n     - Resolve against `--idea-dir` (or the spec's parent directory): `local_source = <idea_dir> / <tail>`.\n     - If the remote path is absolute but outside `spec.repo.workspace`, or relative, resolve against `--idea-dir` directly.\n   - If `local_source` does not exist, raise `CliError(\"missing_idea_file\", \"milestone 'X' idea not found on disk at <local_source>. Use --idea-dir to point at the directory containing ideas.\")`. Mirrors the remote validator's shape but with an actionable hint.\n   - `provider.upload_file(local_source, remote_path)` for every idea file. Also `provider.upload_file(spec_path, remote_spec_path)` where `remote_spec_path = f\"{spec.repo.workspace}/chain.yaml\"`.\n   - Then `provider.ssh_exec(f\"cd {shlex.quote(spec.repo.workspace)} && megaplan chain start --spec {shlex.quote(remote_spec_path)}\")`.\n3. **Persist chain marker (FLAG-006, callers):** after a successful `cloud chain` invocation, write `{ \"remote_spec\": <remote_spec_path>, \"started_at\": <utc_iso> }` to `<persistent_deploy_dir>/last_chain.json`. This gives `cloud status --chain` a stable caller-visible way to discover the remote spec path for ad-hoc chains without requiring `cloud.yaml.mode == \"chain\"`.\n4. **`cloud bootstrap`:**\n   - Upload idea file to `{workspace}/idea.txt`.\n   - Build the remote command, passing `--name` only when `args.plan_name` is set:\n     ```python\n     cmd = f\"cd {wx} && megaplan init --project-dir {wx} --idea-file {ideaq} --auto-start --robustness {rq}\"\n     if args.plan_name:\n         cmd += f\" --name {shlex.quote(args.plan_name)}\"\n     ```\n   - `provider.ssh_exec(cmd)`; relay output.\n5. **Cloud-side isolation invariant preserved:** `chain.load_spec` is used for enumeration only; no chain-state parsing in cloud code.\n\n### Step 16: `cloud status --chain` with marker → fallback → override (`megaplan/cloud/cli.py`) — resolves FLAG-006, issue_hints-1, callers\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **Add** `--chain` + `--remote-spec <path>` flags to the existing `cloud status` subparser.\n2. **Resolve the remote spec path** in this precedence order:\n   1. Explicit `--remote-spec` argument (highest precedence).\n   2. `<persistent_deploy_dir>/last_chain.json::remote_spec` if the marker exists.\n   3. `spec.chain.spec` from `cloud.yaml` when `spec.mode == \"chain\"` (original Rev 2 path).\n   4. Otherwise, `CliError(\"missing_remote_spec\", \"Unable to locate remote chain spec. Pass --remote-spec <path>, run `cloud chain <spec>` first, or set mode: chain in cloud.yaml.\")`.\n3. **Implementation:** fetch the remote chain state via `provider.read_remote_file(chain._state_path_for(Path(remote_spec)))`. Parse with `ChainState.from_dict(json.loads(...))`. Fetch the remote spec YAML, write to a tempfile locally, parse with `chain.load_spec(...)`. Call `chain.format_chain_status(spec, state)`. Emit structured + pretty payload matching the local `chain status` output.\n4. **Contract test** (`tests/test_cloud_chain_status.py`):\n   - Mock `provider.read_remote_file`; assert the three resolution branches each produce the right `remote_spec` path.\n   - Assert the rendered payload is **structurally identical** to local `chain status --spec <same inputs>` (guardrail for \"no cloud-specific chain status parser\").\n\n### Step 17: Secret redaction incl. follow-mode streaming (`megaplan/cloud/redact.py`, `megaplan/cloud/providers/*`) — unchanged from Rev 2\n**Scope:** Medium\n1. `redact(text, secret_names, env=None)` — literal values (len ≥ 8), `NAME=value` / `NAME: value` patterns, and `sk-.../ghp_.../xoxb-...` regexes → `***REDACTED***`.\n2. `stream_redact(proc, secret_names, env=None)` — line-buffered Popen-iterator wrapper for stdout + stderr; flushes short-read tails on EOF.\n3. Wire at `_relay_output`, `logs(follow=False)` (capture-then-redact), and `logs(follow=True)` (Popen with `stdout=PIPE, stderr=PIPE, bufsize=1, text=True` → `stream_redact`). `attach` stays a documented gap (interactive pty).\n4. `_logs_follow(argv)` helper factored across Railway/Local/SSH.\n5. Tests: `tests/test_cloud_redact.py` (table-driven) + `tests/test_cloud_logs_follow_redacted.py` (Popen-mocked per provider).\n\n### Step 18: Missing-CLI errors with install links (`megaplan/cloud/providers/{base,railway,local,ssh}.py`) — unchanged from Rev 2\n**Scope:** Small\n1. `_missing_cli_error(binary, install_url)` helper in `base.py`.\n2. Wire in constructors: Railway → `https://docs.railway.app/develop/cli`; Local → `https://docs.docker.com/get-docker/`; SSH → \"already bundled with most systems; on Windows install OpenSSH via Optional Features.\"\n3. `tests/test_cloud_provider_unavailable.py` monkey-patches `shutil.which`.\n\n### Step 19: Retire `~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev/` + update `docs/cloud.md` + `README` (`docs/cloud-migration-from-reigh.md`, `docs/cloud.md`, `README.md`) — resolves FLAG-007, issue_hints-2, scope, all_locations\n**Scope:** Small\n1. **Draft** `docs/cloud-migration-from-reigh.md`:\n   - `./.venv/bin/python -m megaplan cloud init --cloud-yaml ./cloud.yaml` (note the launcher).\n   - Copy reigh `.env` values into local env or a `.gitignore`-covered `.env`.\n   - `megaplan cloud build && megaplan cloud deploy`.\n   - `megaplan cloud bootstrap ./idea.txt` (no `--plan-name`) for a tiny parity plan; `megaplan cloud status` → expect `done`.\n   - **Cleanup (safe order — resolves FLAG-007, issue_hints-2):**\n     ```bash\n     # Write the pointer FIRST, then clear the folder contents.\n     cat > ~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev/MIGRATED.md <<'EOF'\n     This folder has been migrated into the built-in `megaplan cloud` subcommand.\n     See docs/cloud-migration-from-reigh.md in the megaplan repo for the runbook.\n     EOF\n     # Remove every sibling but leave the directory + MIGRATED.md intact.\n     find ~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev -mindepth 1 ! -name MIGRATED.md -print0 \\\n       | xargs -0 rm -rf\n     ```\n   The sequence writes the pointer before any deletions so the file always ends up in place.\n2. **Update** `docs/cloud.md` (README line 108–113 points users here as the authoritative cloud reference; resolves scope / all_locations):\n   - Add sections for `provider: local` and `provider: ssh` with spec examples.\n   - Add `cloud chain <spec>` workflow explaining the explicit local↔remote path contract (Step 15) — including the `--idea-dir` flag and the default-to-spec-parent behavior.\n   - Add `cloud bootstrap <idea-file>` showing the no-`--plan-name` form as the default.\n   - Add `cloud status --chain` with precedence order (marker > `spec.chain.spec` > `--remote-spec`).\n   - Document the `toolchains:` block with at least one alias + one custom example.\n   - Note the follow-mode log redaction behavior and the `attach` interactive gap.\n3. **Update** `README.md` \"Cloud runs\" section to briefly mention the new providers + toolchain block and point at `docs/cloud.md` for the full reference (keeps the existing `README.md:108-113` pointer intact).\n4. **Update** `megaplan/cloud/templates/cloud.yaml.tmpl` with a commented `toolchains:` example and commented local/ssh provider sections.\n5. **Deliverable from this plan** = the two docs (`cloud-migration-from-reigh.md`, updated `cloud.md`), the README touch-up, the template comments, and the `cloud bootstrap` flow. The actual `rm -rf` / `MIGRATED.md` is human-executed; the runbook's new order is automation-safe.\n\n---\n\n## Execution Order\n1. **Phase 1 (Steps 1–6).** Core commands + parser isolation + launcher fix. Step 4 (lazy cloud subparser via the new `_register_cloud_subcommands` helper) and Step 5 (`sys.executable -m megaplan`) must land before Step 6's isolation + `cloud --help` tests.\n2. **Phase 2 foundations (Steps 7, 8, 9, 10)** before concrete providers (11, 12).\n3. **Concrete providers (Steps 11, 12) + tests (13)** can land in parallel.\n4. **Toolchain (Step 14)** independent; any time post-Phase 1.\n5. **Phase 3 Step 15** before Step 16 (status reads the marker Step 15 writes). Step 17 after providers exist.\n6. **Step 19 (docs + reigh retirement)** last.\n\n## Validation Order\n1. Core unit tests first: `pytest tests/test_chain.py tests/test_handle_init_idea_file.py tests/test_core_without_cloud.py -x`. Proves core/cloud isolation.\n2. Launcher-path regression: `pytest tests/test_auto.py tests/test_chain.py -x`.\n3. Cloud unit tests: `pytest tests/test_cloud_spec.py tests/test_cloud_persistent_dir.py tests/test_cloud_cli_session.py tests/test_cloud_local.py tests/test_cloud_ssh.py tests/test_cloud_template.py tests/test_cloud_redact.py tests/test_cloud_logs_follow_redacted.py tests/test_cloud_provider_unavailable.py tests/test_cloud_chain_status.py -x`.\n4. Chain wrapper integration: `pytest tests/test_cloud_chain_wrapper.py -x` — asserts `cloud chain <spec>` resolves every milestone's local source via the Step-15 path contract and writes the marker file.\n5. Full suite (`pytest`) — no regression; v0.19.0 `cloud.yaml` surface intact.\n6. `@pytest.mark.slow` smoke: `pytest -m slow tests/test_cloud_local_lifecycle.py` on a host with docker.\n7. Manual end-to-end: `provider: local` + `mode: idle` + `cloud bootstrap ./idea.txt` (no `--plan-name`) → `cloud status --chain` via marker → `cloud destroy`.\n8. Reigh migration — human-run per Step 19 runbook.\n",

Resolves FLAG-003, FLAG-005, correctness-1. (2) **Step 15 path contract**: explicit local↔remote mapping — spec paths are treated as remote, local sources resolved by stripping `spec.repo.workspace` prefix and looking under `--idea-dir` (default: spec's parent). Matches bundled `chain.yaml.example` and `docs/cloud.md` conventions. Resolves FLAG-002, correctness-2. (3) **Steps 15+16 ad-hoc chain transport**: `cloud chain` writes `last_chain.json` marker under the persistent deploy dir; `cloud status --chain` reads marker → falls back to `spec.chain.spec` → accepts `--remote-spec` override. Resolves FLAG-006, issue_hints-1, callers. (4) **Step 19 runbook order**: writes `MIGRATED.md` before clearing, uses `find -mindepth 1 ! -name MIGRATED.md -delete` to preserve the pointer. Resolves FLAG-007, issue_hints-2. (5) **Step 19 docs scope**: `docs/cloud.md` added as an explicit MUST target alongside `README` and `cloud.yaml.tmpl` since it's the authoritative cloud reference per `README.md:108-113`.         Idea:
Sprint 2 of the megaplan cloud feature — extend core megaplan ergonomics, add local + ssh providers, ship toolchain extensibility in cloud.yaml, retire the old reigh-megaplan-dev folder.

### Context
Sprint 1 shipped in v0.19.0 on 2026-04-21: built-in megaplan cloud subcommand (init/build/deploy/status/attach/logs/exec/resume/down/destroy) with a Railway provider, extracted from the ad-hoc setup at ~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev/. Spec schema lives in megaplan/cloud/spec.py, templates in megaplan/cloud/templates/, providers in megaplan/cloud/providers/.

Architectural principle established in sprint 1 and now codified: CORE megaplan owns capabilities, CLOUD subcommands are thin transport wrappers. Do not reinvent chain state parsing in cloud. Do not grow a parallel 'cloud chain *' surface. Core gets the command; cloud wraps it for remote execution.

### Sprint 2 deliverables

Core megaplan additions (must work locally without any cloud dependency):
1. megaplan chain start --spec <path> — validate spec, resolve referenced idea files, kick auto-loop per milestone. Replaces the 'init + auto' pair for chain runs.
2. megaplan chain status --spec <path> — read chain_state.json, pretty-print current_milestone, completed, remaining, per-milestone status. Unit tests against synthetic chain states.
3. megaplan init --idea-file <path> with optional --auto-start flag — read idea from a file instead of the positional CLI arg.

Cloud additions (thin wrappers over the new core commands):
4. megaplan cloud chain <spec.yaml> — upload spec, then cloud exec to call megaplan chain start remotely.
5. megaplan cloud bootstrap <idea-file> — upload idea file, then cloud exec to call megaplan init --idea-file --auto-start remotely.
6. megaplan cloud status --chain — fetch remote chain_state.json, invoke the core chain-status formatter on it. Do NOT reinvent the parser.

New providers:
7. Local provider (docker compose) — fastest iteration target, no Railway/cloud dependency. Becomes the CI-friendly smoke-test target.
8. SSH provider (plain docker-over-ssh) — 'any host with docker + ssh access' story.

Toolchain extensibility:
9. toolchains: block in cloud.yaml — declares one or more toolchains (rust, go, java, or custom with user-provided install snippet). Template renderer appends corresponding Dockerfile stages. Without this, the image is Python/Node only.

Hardening + retirement:
10. Secret redaction in cloud logs output (match known secret name patterns, replace with ***REDACTED***).
11. Clear error messages + documentation link when railway or docker CLI is missing at runtime.
12. ~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev/ migration — deploy cloud with a cloud.yaml matching reigh's current env, run a tiny plan to verify parity, then delete the folder and replace with a MIGRATED.md pointer.

### Hard constraints
- Core commands MUST work locally without any cloud code loaded.
- Cloud wrappers MUST call out to core commands, not reimplement.
- Fly provider is OPTIONAL (skip if time-pressured).
- Preserve v0.19.0's public surface; this is additive.
- No breaking cloud.yaml changes from v0.19.0 (add fields, don't rename).
- Unit tests for every new core command; mocked-subprocess tests for every new provider; smoke test for local provider docker-compose lifecycle.

### Explicit non-goals
- No Kubernetes provider.
- No chain-of-chains.
- No cloud-specific chain status parser (the whole point of the core-first architecture).
- No version bump in this plan — sprint 2 ships as v0.20.0 but the bump + CHANGELOG commit is a separate follow-up.

### References
- Sprint 1 landed in v0.19.0 (2026-04-21). Full CHANGELOG entry in CHANGELOG.md.
- Reigh parity reference: ~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev/ and megaplan/cloud/_reference/ (gitignored local copy of same).
- Verified launcher: ./.venv/bin/python -m megaplan (system python is 3.8, fails on NotRequired).
- Sprint 1 target retrospective: cloud subcommand CLI is already wired via megaplan/cloud/cli.py and dispatched from megaplan/cli.py.

Robustness: standard — feature-rich with architecture decisions to enforce, multiple independent pieces worth independent critique, gate useful for scope discipline.

## Overview

**Goal.** Three new **core** commands (`chain start`, `chain status` pretty-print, `init --idea-file`/`--auto-start`) with zero cloud dependency, three **cloud** wrappers that thin-call them (`cloud chain`, `cloud bootstrap`, `cloud status --chain`), two new **providers** (`local` docker-compose, `ssh` docker-over-ssh), a **toolchains** extensibility block in `cloud.yaml`, full log **secret redaction** (including the default `logs -f` path), clear missing-CLI errors, and retirement of `~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev/`.

**Architectural contract (from sprint 1).** Core owns capability; cloud wraps transport. No re-parsing `chain_state.json` in cloud code.

**What Rev 3 changes vs Rev 2 (surgical — five spots).** Per gate v2 guidance, Rev 3 keeps Rev 2's structure intact and only rewrites the sections that had concrete mechanism bugs:
- **Step 4 parser mechanism** — split `build_cloud_parser` so the lazy path can register cloud subcommands on a *fresh standalone parser*, not reparse tail args against a mismatched shape. (resolves FLAG-003, FLAG-005, correctness-1)
- **Step 15 `cloud chain` path contract** — explicit local↔remote mapping. Spec paths are treated as remote; local sources come from `--idea-dir` (default = spec's parent), matched by stripping the `spec.repo.workspace` prefix. (resolves FLAG-002, correctness-2)
- **Step 15+16 ad-hoc chain transport** — `cloud chain` persists a `last_chain.json` marker under the persistent deploy dir; `cloud status --chain` reads marker → falls back to `spec.chain.spec` → accepts `--remote-spec` override. (resolves FLAG-006, issue_hints-1, callers)
- **Step 19 runbook order** — write `MIGRATED.md` before clearing the folder contents. (resolves FLAG-007, issue_hints-2)
- **`docs/cloud.md` explicitly in scope** — added as a MUST documentation target since README line 108–113 points users there as the authoritative cloud reference. (resolves scope, all_locations)

Everything else from Rev 2 stays untouched.

**Current shape** (unchanged from Rev 2).
- Core: `megaplan/chain.py` has `ChainSpec`/`ChainState`/`load_chain_state`/`validate_paths`, drives via `auto.drive()`. `megaplan/handlers.py:handle_init` takes `idea` as a positional arg only.
- Cloud: `megaplan/cloud/{cli.py,spec.py,template.py,providers/base.py,providers/railway.py}`. `spec.py:167-172` rejects non-railway providers. `providers/base.py:52-54` has a hardcoded `if name != "railway"` guard. `cli.py` uses `TemporaryDirectory` for deploy dirs. `build_cloud_parser(subparsers)` currently adds a top-level `cloud` entry under the parent subparsers and then registers subcommands on it (`megaplan/cloud/cli.py:22-27`).

**Constraints** (unchanged).
- No core module imports anything from `megaplan.cloud` (verified by both code + test).
- `cloud.yaml` v0.19.0 spec keeps loading unchanged; all additions are additive.
- Fly provider skipped (brief: optional).
- No version bump / CHANGELOG in this plan — separate follow-up commit.

## Phase 1: Core-first commands + parser & launcher hygiene

### Step 1: Add `megaplan chain start` subcommand (`megaplan/chain.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Extend** `build_chain_parser` (`megaplan/chain.py:528`) with a `start` subparser under `chain_sub`. Flags: `--spec <path>` required, `--no-git-refresh`. Keep the bare `megaplan chain --spec` path working for backcompat with existing wrappers.
2. **Wire** `run_chain_cli` (`megaplan/chain.py:558`) to dispatch `chain start` to the same `run_chain(...)` driver the bare command uses.
3. **Update** `megaplan/cloud/wrappers/mp-chain` to call `megaplan chain start --spec "$1"`.

### Step 2: Add `chain status` pretty-print formatter (`megaplan/chain.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `format_chain_status(spec: ChainSpec, state: ChainState) -> dict` in `megaplan/chain.py` returning `current_milestone` (`{label, index}` or `None`), `completed`, `remaining`, `per_milestone` (status ∈ `completed | in_progress | pending`), plus pass-through `seed_plan`/`current_plan_name`/`last_state`.
2. **Rewrite** the `chain status` branch in `run_chain_cli`: emit the structured JSON to stdout (new `summary` key = formatter output; existing `success`/`spec`/`milestone_count`/`seed_plan`/`chain_state` keys preserved) and a human-readable block to stderr via `writer`.
3. **Export** `format_chain_status` so `cloud status --chain` (Step 16) reuses it verbatim.

### Step 3: `megaplan init --idea-file` with optional `--auto-start` (`megaplan/cli.py`, `megaplan/handlers.py`)
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Adjust** `init_parser` (`megaplan/cli.py:835-852`): make `idea` positional optional (`nargs="?"`); add `--idea-file <path>` and `--auto-start`.
2. **Update** `handle_init` (`megaplan/handlers.py:728`). At the top, compute `idea_text: str`:
   - Both positional + `--idea-file` → `CliError("invalid_args", "--idea-file is mutually exclusive with the positional idea argument")`.
   - `--idea-file`: read UTF-8, strip; empty → `CliError("invalid_args", "idea file is empty")`.
   - Positional: use `args.idea`.
   - Neither: `CliError("invalid_args", "either positional idea or --idea-file is required")`.
   Replace **every** `args.idea` reference (currently `handlers.py:769` `slugify(args.idea)` and `:777` `"idea": args.idea`) with `idea_text`.
3. **`--auto-start` path:** after `save_state(...)` and the init response is built, call `megaplan.auto.drive(plan_name, cwd=root, ...)` in-process with default driver knobs; embed `DriverOutcome` fields under `auto_outcome` in the response.

### Step 4: Lazy cloud subparser registration with a refactored helper (`megaplan/cli.py`, `megaplan/cloud/cli.py`) — resolves FLAG-003, FLAG-005, correctness-1, scope
**Scope:** Small
1. **Refactor** `megaplan/cloud/cli.py::build_cloud_parser`. Split it into two functions that share the subcommand registration body:
   ```python
   def _register_cloud_subcommands(cloud_parser: argparse.ArgumentParser) -> None:
       # The current body of build_cloud_parser from "cloud_sub = cloud_parser.add_subparsers(...)"
       # downward — all subcommand registrations (init/build/deploy/status/attach/logs/exec/resume/down/destroy
       # plus the new chain/bootstrap from Step 15).
       ...

   def build_cloud_parser(subparsers: Any) -> None:
       cloud_parser = subparsers.add_parser("cloud", help="Manage provider-backed megaplan cloud runners")
       _register_cloud_subcommands(cloud_parser)
   ```
   This preserves the existing under-subparsers API for anyone who uses it (today only `megaplan/cli.py`); the new `_register_cloud_subcommands` can register on any parser object.
2. **Replace** the unconditional import at `megaplan/cli.py:1006-1007` with a stub + deferred dispatch:
   - In `build_parser`, register a stub `cloud` entry that swallows all remaining args: `cloud_stub = subparsers.add_parser("cloud", help="Provider-backed megaplan cloud runners (loaded on demand)", add_help=False); cloud_stub.add_argument("cloud_args", nargs=argparse.REMAINDER)`. The stub parses but does not dispatch.
   - In `main`, **intercept before `parser.parse_known_args`**: if `argv and argv[0] == "cloud"`, lazy-import `from megaplan.cloud.cli import _register_cloud_subcommands, run_cloud_cli`, build a fresh standalone `cloud_parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="megaplan cloud")`, call `_register_cloud_subcommands(cloud_parser)`, then `cloud_args = cloud_parser.parse_args(argv[1:])`. Dispatch via `run_cloud_cli(root, cloud_args)`. This bypasses the top-level parser entirely for the cloud path — no "reparse tail args against a mismatched parent shape."
3. **`megaplan cloud --help` discoverability:** the early-intercept branch fires for `argv[0] == "cloud"` regardless of what follows, so `["cloud", "--help"]` hits the standalone parser and argparse emits the usual subparser help block listing every cloud subcommand. Test in Step 6 asserts this.
4. **Isolation guarantee:** when `argv[0] != "cloud"`, the stub parser is all that exists; no `megaplan.cloud.*` module is imported. This is what Step 6's isolation test verifies.

### Step 5: Standardize subprocess launcher (`megaplan/auto.py`, `megaplan/chain.py`) — resolves callers (already addressed in Rev 2, unchanged)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Replace** `["megaplan", *args]` at `megaplan/auto.py:97` with `[sys.executable, "-m", "megaplan", *args]`.
2. **Replace** `["megaplan", "init", ...]` / `["megaplan", "status", ...]` at `megaplan/chain.py:323, 271` with the same prefix.
3. **Keep** bare `megaplan` in shell wrappers under `megaplan/cloud/wrappers/` — they run inside the container where `megaplan` is on PATH.
4. **Test update:** any `tests/test_auto.py` / `tests/test_chain.py` argv assertions get the new prefix.

### Step 6: Core tests (`tests/test_chain.py`, `tests/test_handle_init_idea_file.py`, `tests/test_core_without_cloud.py`)
**Scope:** Small
1. **Extend** `tests/test_chain.py`: `test_chain_start_invokes_driver`, `test_format_chain_status_pretty` (seed+3 milestones).
2. **Add** `tests/test_handle_init_idea_file.py`: `test_init_reads_idea_file`, `test_init_rejects_both_positional_and_idea_file`, `test_init_rejects_empty_idea_file`, `test_init_slugify_uses_resolved_idea_text`, `test_init_state_idea_is_file_contents`, `test_init_auto_start_invokes_drive`.
3. **Add** `tests/test_core_without_cloud.py`:
   - Uses a `subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-c", ...])` subprocess with `PYTHONPATH` controlled so the child has no `megaplan.cloud` importable path (monkeypatch or a sitecustomize stub that raises on `megaplan.cloud` import). Runs `main(["chain", "status", "--spec", ...])` and `main(["init", "--project-dir", ..., "--idea-file", ..., "--auto-start"])` — both must succeed.
   - Separate `test_cloud_help_still_discoverable`: runs `main(["cloud", "--help"])`, asserts exit 0 and the output mentions every cloud subcommand (existing + new `chain`/`bootstrap`).

## Phase 2: Cloud infrastructure refactors + providers

### Step 7: Persistent per-project deploy-dir state (`megaplan/cloud/cli.py`) — unchanged from Rev 2
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Introduce** `_persistent_deploy_dir(spec: CloudSpec) -> Path`:
   - Railway → `TemporaryDirectory` context (stateless; `railway up` pushes).
   - Local → `~/.megaplan/cloud/<compose_project>/` (mkdir -p), persistent.
   - SSH → `~/.megaplan/cloud/ssh-<host>/`, persistent.
2. **Refactor** `run_cloud_cli` so `_materialized_deploy_dir` is provider-aware. `destroy` is the only command that clears the persistent dir (after down succeeds).
3. **Dispatch change:** local/ssh actions (`build`/`deploy`/`exec`/`logs`/`down`/`destroy`) all work from the same stable dir so `docker-compose.yaml` and `.env` are always findable.
4. **Test** (`tests/test_cloud_persistent_dir.py`): `build → exec → down` on a `provider: local` spec reuses the same materialized dir.

### Step 8: Generalize session/attach glue (`megaplan/cloud/cli.py`) — unchanged from Rev 2
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `Provider.supports_session: bool` class attribute. Railway overrides to `True`; Local/SSH inherit `False`.
2. **Enforce** in `run_cloud_cli`: `getattr(args, "session", None)` + `not provider.supports_session` → `CliError("invalid_args", "--session is only supported for provider: railway")`.
3. **Test** (`tests/test_cloud_cli_session.py`).

### Step 9: Unlock `local` and `ssh` providers in the spec loader (`megaplan/cloud/spec.py`) — unchanged from Rev 2
**Scope:** Small
1. **Accept** `"railway" | "local" | "ssh"` in `load_spec`. `FUTURE_PROVIDERS = ("fly",)` with a distinct error ("Fly provider is deferred to a future sprint; use railway, local, or ssh for now.").
2. **Add** `LocalSpec(compose_project, workdir)` and `SshSpec(host, user, port, identity_file, remote_dir, container)` dataclasses; parse when provider matches.
3. **Add** `local: LocalSpec | None = None` / `ssh: SshSpec | None = None` to `CloudSpec`.
4. **Update tests:** drop reject-sprint-2 test; add happy paths + Fly future-provider error test.

### Step 10: `Provider` base + registry + file-transfer hooks (`megaplan/cloud/providers/base.py`, `providers/railway.py`) — unchanged from Rev 2
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `Provider.upload_file(src, dest)` and `Provider.read_remote_file(path)` hooks — base raises `CliError("not_implemented", ...)`; every provider overrides.
2. **Replace** `providers/base.py:52-54` hardcoded guard with a lazy `{"railway": ..., "local": ..., "ssh": ...}` factory dict.
3. **Implement** on `RailwayProvider`: `railway ssh -- "base64 -d > dest"` (stdin base64) for upload; `railway ssh -- cat <path>` for read.

### Step 11: Local provider (`megaplan/cloud/providers/local.py`, `megaplan/cloud/templates/docker-compose.yaml.tmpl`) — unchanged from Rev 2
**Scope:** Medium
1. Single-container `agent` service in `docker-compose.yaml.tmpl`, bind-mounted `./workspace` inside the persistent deploy dir, env via `.env`, port per `resources.port`.
2. `LocalProvider` implements every ABC method with `docker compose -p <proj> -f <deploy>/docker-compose.yaml ...`. All operate on the persistent deploy dir from Step 7.
3. Guard construction: missing `docker` → `CliError("provider_unavailable", ...)` with install link (Step 18 standardizes).
4. `template.py` materializes `docker-compose.yaml` when `spec.provider == "local"`.

### Step 12: SSH provider (`megaplan/cloud/providers/ssh.py`) — unchanged from Rev 2
**Scope:** Medium
1. `SshProvider` reads `SshSpec`; `_remote_run(cmd)` uses `ssh [-i] [-p] user@host -- bash -lc <shlex.quote(cmd)>`.
2. `build` rsyncs the persistent deploy dir to `remote_dir`, then `docker build` remotely. `scp -r` fallback if rsync missing (warn, not fail).
3. `deploy` writes `.env` remotely, `docker run -d --name <container> --env-file ... -v ... -p ...`.
4. All methods follow Railway's pattern over `_remote_run`; `shlex.quote` every interpolated user value.

**Scope:** Medium
1. Mocked-subprocess unit tests — exact argv assertions.
2. `@pytest.mark.slow` smoke test: materialize → build → deploy → `ssh_exec("which megaplan")` → destroy. Under 5 minutes.

### Step 14: Toolchain extensibility (`megaplan/cloud/spec.py`, `megaplan/cloud/template.py`, `megaplan/cloud/templates/Dockerfile`) — unchanged from Rev 2
**Scope:** Medium
1. `toolchains:` accepts string aliases (`rust`/`go`/`java`) or `{name, install}` mappings.
2. `TOOLCHAIN_BLOCK` placeholder in the Dockerfile between Node install and `COPY entrypoint.sh`; `_TOOLCHAIN_RECIPES` dict in `template.py`.

4. Tests: rust recipe, custom snippet, unknown alias rejection, and a byte-identical-baseline diff for the no-toolchain path.

## Phase 3: Cloud wrappers + hardening + migration

### Step 15: `cloud chain` + `cloud bootstrap` with explicit path contract and marker persistence (`megaplan/cloud/cli.py`) — resolves FLAG-002, FLAG-006, correctness-2, issue_hints-1, callers
**Scope:** Medium
1. **Add** subparsers:
   - `chain` — positional `spec` (local path), optional `--idea-dir <local>` (default: spec's parent directory).
   - `bootstrap` — positional `idea_file` (local path), optional `--plan-name` (omit → core slug default), optional `--robustness` (default `standard`).
2. **`cloud chain` explicit path contract (FLAG-002, correctness-2):**
   - Parse the spec locally with `megaplan.chain.load_spec(spec_path)` — **enumeration only**; no reimplementation.
   - **Spec paths are treated as remote paths** (matches `megaplan/cloud/templates/chain.yaml.example` + `docs/cloud.md`). The bundled example uses `/workspace/ideas/m1.txt` style, and that remains canonical.
   - For each milestone, compute the **local source path** by:
     - If the remote path begins with `spec.repo.workspace + "/"`, strip that prefix → relative tail (e.g. `/workspace/ideas/m1.txt` with `workspace: /workspace` → `ideas/m1.txt`).
     - Resolve against `--idea-dir` (or the spec's parent directory): `local_source = <idea_dir> / <tail>`.
     - If the remote path is absolute but outside `spec.repo.workspace`, or relative, resolve against `--idea-dir` directly.
   - If `local_source` does not exist, raise `CliError("missing_idea_file", "milestone 'X' idea not found on disk at <local_source>. Use --idea-dir to point at the directory containing ideas.")`. Mirrors the remote validator's shape but with an actionable hint.
   - `provider.upload_file(local_source, remote_path)` for every idea file. Also `provider.upload_file(spec_path, remote_spec_path)` where `remote_spec_path = f"{spec.repo.workspace}/chain.yaml"`.
   - Then `provider.ssh_exec(f"cd {shlex.quote(spec.repo.workspace)} && megaplan chain start --spec {shlex.quote(remote_spec_path)}")`.
3. **Persist chain marker (FLAG-006, callers):** after a successful `cloud chain` invocation, write `{ "remote_spec": <remote_spec_path>, "started_at": <utc_iso> }` to `<persistent_deploy_dir>/last_chain.json`. This gives `cloud status --chain` a stable caller-visible way to discover the remote spec path for ad-hoc chains without requiring `cloud.yaml.mode == "chain"`.
4. **`cloud bootstrap`:**
   - Upload idea file to `{workspace}/idea.txt`.
   - Build the remote command, passing `--name` only when `args.plan_name` is set:
     ```python
     cmd = f"cd {wx} && megaplan init --project-dir {wx} --idea-file {ideaq} --auto-start --robustness {rq}"
     if args.plan_name:
         cmd += f" --name {shlex.quote(args.plan_name)}"
     ```
   - `provider.ssh_exec(cmd)`; relay output.
5. **Cloud-side isolation invariant preserved:** `chain.load_spec` is used for enumeration only; no chain-state parsing in cloud code.

### Step 16: `cloud status --chain` with marker → fallback → override (`megaplan/cloud/cli.py`) — resolves FLAG-006, issue_hints-1, callers
**Scope:** Small
1. **Add** `--chain` + `--remote-spec <path>` flags to the existing `cloud status` subparser.
2. **Resolve the remote spec path** in this precedence order:
   1. Explicit `--remote-spec` argument (highest precedence).
   2. `<persistent_deploy_dir>/last_chain.json::remote_spec` if the marker exists.
   3. `spec.chain.spec` from `cloud.yaml` when `spec.mode == "chain"` (original Rev 2 path).
   4. Otherwise, `CliError("missing_remote_spec", "Unable to locate remote chain spec. Pass --remote-spec <path>, run `cloud chain <spec>` first, or set mode: chain in cloud.yaml.")`.
3. **Implementation:** fetch the remote chain state via `provider.read_remote_file(chain._state_path_for(Path(remote_spec)))`. Parse with `ChainState.from_dict(json.loads(...))`. Fetch the remote spec YAML, write to a tempfile locally, parse with `chain.load_spec(...)`. Call `chain.format_chain_status(spec, state)`. Emit structured + pretty payload matching the local `chain status` output.
4. **Contract test** (`tests/test_cloud_chain_status.py`):
   - Mock `provider.read_remote_file`; assert the three resolution branches each produce the right `remote_spec` path.
   - Assert the rendered payload is **structurally identical** to local `chain status --spec <same inputs>` (guardrail for "no cloud-specific chain status parser").

### Step 17: Secret redaction incl. follow-mode streaming (`megaplan/cloud/redact.py`, `megaplan/cloud/providers/*`) — unchanged from Rev 2
**Scope:** Medium
1. `redact(text, secret_names, env=None)` — literal values (len ≥ 8), `NAME=value` / `NAME: value` patterns, and `sk-.../ghp_.../xoxb-...` regexes → `***REDACTED***`.
2. `stream_redact(proc, secret_names, env=None)` — line-buffered Popen-iterator wrapper for stdout + stderr; flushes short-read tails on EOF.
3. Wire at `_relay_output`, `logs(follow=False)` (capture-then-redact), and `logs(follow=True)` (Popen with `stdout=PIPE, stderr=PIPE, bufsize=1, text=True` → `stream_redact`). `attach` stays a documented gap (interactive pty).
4. `_logs_follow(argv)` helper factored across Railway/Local/SSH.
5. Tests: `tests/test_cloud_redact.py` (table-driven) + `tests/test_cloud_logs_follow_redacted.py` (Popen-mocked per provider).

### Step 18: Missing-CLI errors with install links (`megaplan/cloud/providers/{base,railway,local,ssh}.py`) — unchanged from Rev 2
**Scope:** Small
1. `_missing_cli_error(binary, install_url)` helper in `base.py`.
2. Wire in constructors: Railway → `https://docs.railway.app/develop/cli`; Local → `https://docs.docker.com/get-docker/`; SSH → "already bundled with most systems; on Windows install OpenSSH via Optional Features."
3. `tests/test_cloud_provider_unavailable.py` monkey-patches `shutil.which`.

### Step 19: Retire `~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev/` + update `docs/cloud.md` + `README` (`docs/cloud-migration-from-reigh.md`, `docs/cloud.md`, `README.md`) — resolves FLAG-007, issue_hints-2, scope, all_locations
**Scope:** Small
1. **Draft** `docs/cloud-migration-from-reigh.md`:
   - `./.venv/bin/python -m megaplan cloud init --cloud-yaml ./cloud.yaml` (note the launcher).
   - Copy reigh `.env` values into local env or a `.gitignore`-covered `.env`.
   - `megaplan cloud build && megaplan cloud deploy`.
   - `megaplan cloud bootstrap ./idea.txt` (no `--plan-name`) for a tiny parity plan; `megaplan cloud status` → expect `done`.
   - **Cleanup (safe order — resolves FLAG-007, issue_hints-2):**
     ```bash
     # Write the pointer FIRST, then clear the folder contents.
     cat > ~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev/MIGRATED.md <<'EOF'
     This folder has been migrated into the built-in `megaplan cloud` subcommand.
     See docs/cloud-migration-from-reigh.md in the megaplan repo for the runbook.
     EOF
     # Remove every sibling but leave the directory + MIGRATED.md intact.
     find ~/Documents/reigh-megaplan-dev -mindepth 1 ! -name MIGRATED.md -print0 \
       | xargs -0 rm -rf
     ```
   The sequence writes the pointer before any deletions so the file always ends up in place.
2. **Update** `docs/cloud.md` (README line 108–113 points users here as the authoritative cloud reference; resolves scope / all_locations):
   - Add sections for `provider: local` and `provider: ssh` with spec examples.
   - Add `cloud chain <spec>` workflow explaining the explicit local↔remote path contract (Step 15) — including the `--idea-dir` flag and the default-to-spec-parent behavior.
   - Add `cloud bootstrap <idea-file>` showing the no-`--plan-name` form as the default.
   - Add `cloud status --chain` with precedence order (marker > `spec.chain.spec` > `--remote-spec`).
   - Document the `toolchains:` block with at least one alias + one custom example.
   - Note the follow-mode log redaction behavior and the `attach` interactive gap.
3. **Update** `README.md` "Cloud runs" section to briefly mention the new providers + toolchain block and point at `docs/cloud.md` for the full reference (keeps the existing `README.md:108-113` pointer intact).
4. **Update** `megaplan/cloud/templates/cloud.yaml.tmpl` with a commented `toolchains:` example and commented local/ssh provider sections.
5. **Deliverable from this plan** = the two docs (`cloud-migration-from-reigh.md`, updated `cloud.md`), the README touch-up, the template comments, and the `cloud bootstrap` flow. The actual `rm -rf` / `MIGRATED.md` is human-executed; the runbook's new order is automation-safe.

---

## Execution Order
1. **Phase 1 (Steps 1–6).** Core commands + parser isolation + launcher fix. Step 4 (lazy cloud subparser via the new `_register_cloud_subcommands` helper) and Step 5 (`sys.executable -m megaplan`) must land before Step 6's isolation + `cloud --help` tests.
2. **Phase 2 foundations (Steps 7, 8, 9, 10)** before concrete providers (11, 12).
3. **Concrete providers (Steps 11, 12) + tests (13)** can land in parallel.
4. **Toolchain (Step 14)** independent; any time post-Phase 1.
5. **Phase 3 Step 15** before Step 16 (status reads the marker Step 15 writes). Step 17 after providers exist.
6. **Step 19 (docs + reigh retirement)** last.

## Validation Order
1. Core unit tests first: `pytest tests/test_chain.py tests/test_handle_init_idea_file.py tests/test_core_without_cloud.py -x`. Proves core/cloud isolation.
2. Launcher-path regression: `pytest tests/test_auto.py tests/test_chain.py -x`.
3. Cloud unit tests: `pytest tests/test_cloud_spec.py tests/test_cloud_persistent_dir.py tests/test_cloud_cli_session.py tests/test_cloud_local.py tests/test_cloud_ssh.py tests/test_cloud_template.py tests/test_cloud_redact.py tests/test_cloud_logs_follow_redacted.py tests/test_cloud_provider_unavailable.py tests/test_cloud_chain_status.py -x`.
4. Chain wrapper integration: `pytest tests/test_cloud_chain_wrapper.py -x` — asserts `cloud chain <spec>` resolves every milestone's local source via the Step-15 path contract and writes the marker file.
5. Full suite (`pytest`) — no regression; v0.19.0 `cloud.yaml` surface intact.
6. `@pytest.mark.slow` smoke: `pytest -m slow tests/test_cloud_local_lifecycle.py` on a host with docker.
7. Manual end-to-end: `provider: local` + `mode: idle` + `cloud bootstrap ./idea.txt` (no `--plan-name`) → `cloud status --chain` via marker → `cloud destroy`.
8. Reigh migration — human-run per Step 19 runbook.
